### Key Findings
- The plan text still assumes `ratatui 0.30`, but the workspace is pinned to `ratatui 0.25` in `Cargo.toml:89-91`.

## Plan 05: Terminal Widget Library — Completed 2026-03-19

### Types Defined
- `BrailleSparkline` — `bardo_terminal::widgets::sparkline` — braille-pattern sparkline for dense single-row traces
- `VitalityGauge` — `bardo_terminal::widgets::gauge` — phase-colored vitality bar
- `ConfidenceGauge` — `bardo_terminal::widgets::gauge` — threshold-colored confidence bar
- `AccuracyGauge` — `bardo_terminal::widgets::gauge` — threshold-colored prediction accuracy bar
- `MockPhase` — `bardo_terminal::widgets::gauge` — placeholder vitality phase until `golem_core::BehavioralPhase` is wired in
- `PheromoneHeatmap` — `bardo_terminal::widgets::heatmap` — Viridis-style 2D signal grid with layer tinting and pulse overrides
- `PheromoneLayer` — `bardo_terminal::widgets::heatmap` — `Threat` / `Opportunity` / `Wisdom` layer tag
- `TimelineRibbon` — `bardo_terminal::widgets::timeline` — horizontal event ribbon over a tick window
- `TimelineEvent` — `bardo_terminal::widgets::timeline` — one event marker on the ribbon
- `RibbonEventType` — `bardo_terminal::widgets::timeline` — trade / dream / phase / anomaly / death event class
- `EventFeed` — `bardo_terminal::widgets::feed` — bounded VecDeque-backed log with substring filter
- `FeedEntry` — `bardo_terminal::widgets::feed` — one log entry carrying tick, level, and message
- `FeedLevel` — `bardo_terminal::widgets::feed` — `Info` / `Warn` / `Error` / `Debug` feed severity
- `TabBar` — `bardo_terminal::widgets::tabs` — horizontal tab strip with active highlighting
- `StatusBar` — `bardo_terminal::widgets::status_bar` — standalone bottom-row status line widget
- `ScrollableList` — `bardo_terminal::widgets::scrolllist` — cursor-driven list with substring filtering helpers
- `KeyHelpOverlay` — `bardo_terminal::widgets::key_help` — centered floating keybinding overlay
- `KeyBinding` — `bardo_terminal::widgets::key_help` — key + description row for the overlay

### Public Traits
- None

### Deviations
- Targeted `ratatui 0.25.0`, not the plan’s `0.30` snippets. Widgets use `Buffer::get_mut` plus explicit bounds checks because `cell_mut((x, y))` is unavailable in `0.25`.
- `MockPhase` documents future replacement with `golem_core::BehavioralPhase`, matching the workspace’s actual phase type location rather than the plan text’s `golem_mortality` path.
- `TimelineRibbon` renders a neutral track between events because the planned API exposes no phase-series input to color the track segments.
- `StatusBar` was implemented as a standalone widget but intentionally not wired into `App::render`; the existing footer remains in place to avoid changing scaffold chrome outside this plan’s stated scope.
- Several widgets still allocate short `String`/`Vec` values in render-adjacent code paths (labels, truncation, filtering). The widget library is functional, but the plan’s no-allocation hot-path goal is only partially met at scaffold stage.
- `docs/src/apps/bardo-terminal.md` was cleaned up to read as a user-facing guide and no longer leaks internal source paths.

### Unresolved Issues
- No screen consumes the new widgets yet; they are available through `crate::widgets::*` for follow-on screen plans.
- `TabBar` does not implement the plan prose’s compact-layout dot-collapse behavior because its API does not receive a breakpoint or compact flag.
- The manual sparkline exercise snippet from the plan was not patched into `HomeScreen`; verification used unit tests plus a full interactive app smoke test instead.

### Status
- Branch: codex/plan/05-terminal-widgets
- cargo check: pass
- cargo test: 91 pass, 0 fail, 0 ignored
- This pass: docs cleanup and verification rerun

## Plan 04: Terminal Scaffold & Render Loop — Completed 2026-03-19

### Types Defined
- `App` — `bardo_terminal::app` — owns terminal state, the screen registry, and the render loop
- `AppAction` — `bardo_terminal::state` — quit / next-screen / prev-screen / resize actions
- `AppState` — `bardo_terminal::state` — tick count, connection status, vitality placeholder, layout breakpoint

- `ratatui` was pinned to `0.25.0` in the workspace because `0.29` and `0.28` pulled a Rust-1.88-only proc-macro path in this toolchain (`rustc 1.85.1`).

- `ColorPalette` is a zero-sized namespace marker with module constants, not a data-bearing palette struct.
- `App::run` borrows the terminal and `main.rs` performs teardown explicitly after the loop exits.

- No runtime data is wired yet; vitality, connectivity, and most screens remain placeholders.
- The remaining 28 screens are still stub renderers.

`PositionSizer` — `golem_safety::risk` — Kelly criterion: f = f* × mortality_scalar × uncertainty_scalar; capped at max_single_position_pct — Plan 59
`ReturnEstimator` — `golem_safety::risk` — EWM return estimator: alpha=0.05, min_obs=30; mean/variance/estimation_variance — Plan 59

`VaRCalculator` — `golem_safety::risk` — parametric VaR_95 = position_usd × annualized_vol × 1.645 / sqrt(252); ceiling on Kelly size — Plan 59
`AdaptiveRiskAdjuster` — `golem_safety::risk` — modulates RiskProfile by BehavioralPhase: Conservation → position_limit_usd=0, Terminal → 10% limits — Plan 59
`BayesianConfidence` — `golem_safety::risk` — Beta(2,2) prior; success=+alpha, failure=+beta×1.5(volatile)×2.0(drawdown); 3-failure regression — Plan 59
`DeploymentStage` — `golem_safety::risk` — Shadow(<0.30)/Micro(0.30-0.50, 2%)/Scaled(0.50-0.70, 10%)/Full(>0.70) — Plan 59

`RiskInput` — `golem_safety::risk` — requested_usd, portfolio_usd, current_nav_usd, annualized_volatility, remaining_ticks, behavioral_phase, somatic_marker — Plan 59
`LayerResult` — `golem_safety::risk` — layer u8, passed bool, adjustment Option<f64>, notes Vec<String> — Plan 59
`RiskError` — `golem_safety::risk` — SomaticUnavailable / MissingPortfolioState / Other — Plan 59

`GateResult` — `golem_safety::action_gate` — approved bool, warden_delay_override Option<u64>, block_reason Option<String>, recommended_position_size Option<f64> — Plan 59

`TaCorticalExtension` — `golem_ta::cortical` — 8 f32 signals at #[repr(C, align(64))], 64 bytes; trend_direction/trend_strength/volatility_regime/liquidity_score/pattern_match_confidence/cross_protocol_divergence/momentum_zscore/ta_surprise — Plan 75a

`ConvolutionWindow` — `golem_ta::temporal` — window state: depth, filled, current pattern — Plan 75a
`CrossProtocolBinder` — `golem_ta::cross_protocol` — binds Uniswap/Aave/Morpho state hypervectors into composite; returns (composite, cross_protocol_divergence f32) — Plan 75a

`PatternLibrary` — `golem_ta::pattern` — stores up to 256 named BscHypervector patterns; match_pattern/top_k by cosine similarity — Plan 75a
`PatternMatch` — `golem_ta::pattern` — label: String, similarity: f64, confidence: f32 — Plan 75a
`TaCorticalExtensionWriter` — `golem_ta::writer` — heartbeat-driven; consumes WitnessStream + ProtocolStateEngine → updates Arc<Mutex<TaCorticalExtension>> each Gamma tick — Plan 75a
`TaFeelingsBinder` — `golem_ta::feelings` — maps TaCorticalExtension signals to Vec<SomaticMarker> for Daimon; 6-condition rule table — Plan 75a
`TaConfig` — `golem_ta::config` — TOML config: window_depth (default 20), pattern_match_threshold (default 0.4), event_change_threshold (default 0.05), cross_protocol_chains — Plan 75a
`TaError` — `golem_ta::error` — crate-wide thiserror error enum — Plan 75a

- `golem-grimoire` — owns: LanceDB episodic store, SQLite semantic store, PLAYBOOK.md procedural store, four-factor retrieval, admission gate, decay, EmbeddingDriftDetector, HybridRetriever (ANN+BM25+RRF+MMR), GrimoireAdmissionGate (A-MAC five-factor), ArtifactQualityScorer, RevisionApplier (12 revisions), RegimeTag — Plans 12, 69
- `golem-daimon` — owns: ALMA emotion model, PAD vectors, appraisal engine, somatic markers, mood congruence, clade emotional contagion — Plan 14a
- `golem-mortality` — owns: economic clock, epistemic clock, stochastic clock, VitalityState, behavioral phases, thanatopsis — Plans 13a, 13b

- `golem-hypnagogia` — owns: hypnagogic/hypnopompic state machines, DaliInterrupt, xenocognition, inner worlds — Plan 25

- `golem-inference` — owns: gateway client types, T0/T1/T2 tier definitions, InferenceClient trait, SSE parser — Plan 11

- `golem-chain-intelligence` — owns: WitnessEngine (block streaming), NormalizedBlock/Tx, AbiRegistry (ABI decoding), EventRouter, WitnessStream API, Indexer (sled, optional), ProtocolStateEngine (hot/warm/cold layers), ChainScope (BinaryFuse8 + scored interest list) — Plans 17, 18

### App Binaries (in `apps/`)

- `bardo-gateway` — owns: Axum HTTP server for LLM inference routing, SSE streaming, L1-L8 caching/safety middleware, provider backends (5), x402 payments, budget enforcement, session service, inference profiles, multi-model orchestration, sheaf observation, metrics, Venice private cognition (SecurityClass/DiemTracker), BankrBackend (MetabolicLoop/CrossModelVerification), knowledge paywall handler, auth (ApiKey/JWT/RBAC/SessionStore), portal onboarding types (PortalWizardState/PortalSession), runtime interaction model (InteractionMode/ModeController), DeFi activity log (ActivityLog), webhook dispatcher (HMAC-SHA256), data visibility filter (PrivacyFilter 4-tier), knowledge browser API, public stats gateway, observability (Prometheus metrics/health probes), state model (PersistentState/CheckpointManager), real-time subscriptions (EventBus/EventPublisher/WS/SSE), UX error types (UxError/ErrorResponse) — Plans 11, 36-39c, 53, 54, 55, 60, 63, 63a, 63b, 65
- `bardo-terminal` — owns: ratatui TUI application, screen system, render loop, widget library, 5-step onboarding wizard, Solaris constellation (SolarisScreen/ConstellationView), cybernetic loop (CyberneticLoopScreen), DataBridge/DataHandles (live data subscription layer), HEARTH screens (Home/Portfolio/Activity/Settings), MIND screens (Cognition/Knowledge/Dreams/Predictions/Insights), SOMA screens (Vitality/Emotions/Mortality/Health/Body), WORLD screens (Chain/Markets/Protocols/Events), FATE screens (Trading/Positions/Strategies/Risk/Death/Legacy), COMMAND screens (Config/Agents/Skills/System), DebugPanel (F12, 5 modes) — Plans 04-08d, 44, 63, 66, 70a-70c
- `bardo-styx` — owns: WebSocket clade sync service, Grimoire backup, knowledge relay — Plan 40
- `bardo-compute` — owns: Fly.io VM provisioning (VmTier/WarmPool/Machine), x402 per-hour billing (BillingRecord/BillingCycle/PaymentCollector), fleet management (FleetStats/FleetManager), deployment types (DeploymentPath/ContainerImage/UpgradeStrategy), CLI (provision/deprovision/status/fleet/scale) — Plan 67

### Other

- `apps/mirage-rs/` — owns: in-process revm fork, lazy-latest RPC, CoW state layers, JSON-RPC compat, scenario runner — Plan 03
- `sidecar/tools-ts/` — owns: Uniswap SDK V3/V4 LP math, JSON-RPC 2.0 over Unix socket — Plan 01 (stub), Plan 26+ (integration)
- `tests/` — owns: conformance, adversarial, property, integration tests — various plans

## Decisions & Deviations
<!-- Numbered. Include WHY. -->
<!-- Format: D-NN: [decision] — because [reason] — Plan NN -->

- D-01: Canonical crate names follow prd2/17-monorepo/00-packages.md, NOT prompt.md dependency graph — because monorepo spec is the authoritative source; prompt.md graph uses older names (bardo-witness→golem-chain-intelligence, bardo-triage→golem-triage) — Plan generator

- D-02: Apps in `apps/` (bardo-gateway, bardo-terminal, bardo-styx, bardo-compute, mirage-rs) are NOT workspace library crates — because they are binaries with their own Cargo.toml, not included in workspace `[members]` as libraries — Plan generator

- D-03: Plans 04-08d (terminal primitives) use placeholder types for runtime data connections — because runtime subsystems (Plans 09-16) don't exist yet; connections wired in Plans 70a-70c — Plan generator
- D-04: Apps (bardo-gateway, bardo-terminal, bardo-styx, bardo-compute, mirage-rs) ARE workspace [members] — confirmed by prd2/17-monorepo/01-rust-workspace.md; resolves Q-01 — Plan 01
- D-05: golem-binary in crates/ is a binary crate (src/main.rs + [[bin]]), not a lib — ships as bardo-golem executable — Plan 01
- D-593: Plan 01 scaffolds 26 workspace members (21 crates + 5 apps), not 25 — because the explicit member list includes `apps/bardo-compute/` even though stale verification/docs text still says 25 — Plan 01
- D-594: `rodio` stays non-optional in `[workspace.dependencies]` — because Cargo forbids optional workspace dependencies; individual crates add `optional = true` when they opt into audio — Plan 01
- D-06: CorticalState uses superset of fields from 00-overview.md + 18-cortical-state.md — both source files consulted — Plan 02
- D-07: GolemEvent.ts stored as ts_millis: u64 (not Instant) — Instant is not Serialize — Plan 02
- D-08: Hook context types defined as empty structs in golem-core; gain fields in golem-runtime (Plan 14b) — Plan 02
- D-09: mirage-rs default port is 8545; test harnesses use 18545 to avoid conflict — Plan 03
- D-10: revm 36.x uses Context::mainnet().with_db(...).build_mainnet() + transact_commit() — Plan 03
- D-11: BytecodeCache shared across HybridDB instances via Arc<Mutex<BytecodeCache>>; never invalidated — Plan 03
- D-12: ERC-8004 write operations deferred to Plan 45; Plan 09 is read-only — Plan 09
- D-13: RevmSimulator delegates to MirageClient in #[cfg(test)] to avoid live RPC — Plan 09
- D-15: AllowList default tokens are Base L2 mainnet addresses — Plan 10
- D-16: ScreenId gains ProtocolViews variant (Plan 07); Plan 04's screen_id_count test must update from 29 to 30 — Plan 07
- D-17: LendingMarketWidget uses raw ratatui Gauge not VitalityGauge — VitalityGauge is phase-specific; utilization needs independent color thresholds — Plan 07
- D-18: Mock protocol types use mock_default() not Default trait — naming signals fake data, reduces risk of shipping mock values — Plan 07
- D-19: BrailleCanvas wraps Plan 05's BrailleSparkline internally; public API unchanged — BrailleCanvas is the general engine, BrailleSparkline a convenience wrapper — Plan 08a
- D-20: rand crate added to bardo-terminal for demoscene RNG — Plan 08a
- D-21: TunnelEffect pre-computes LUTs at init time; App::handle_resize() must reinitialize TunnelEffect on terminal resize — Plan 08a
- D-22: golem-chain-intelligence WS test port is 18546 (HTTP is 18545 per D-09) — avoids port collision when both HTTP and WS test instances run — Plan 17
- D-23: Chain events are GolemEvent variants from golem-core (no separate ChainEvent enum in golem-chain-intelligence) — canonical events are the five Chain* variants already defined in EventPayload — Plan 17
- D-24: golem-chain-intelligence embeds ProtocolStateEngine (Plans 17-18 scope); golem-triage is a separate crate (Plan 19) — per prd2/17-monorepo crate boundary spec — Plan 17
- D-25: ChainScope uses BinaryFuse8 (xorf crate) not a Bloom filter — BinaryFuse8: 8.7 bits/entry vs ~10 for Bloom at equivalent FPR; immutable enables atomic ArcSwap swap with zero downtime — Plan 18
- D-26: Protocol state stores raw on-chain units only (U256 Ray-encoded rates, sqrtPriceX96) — derived values (price_token0_per_token1, APR %) belong in consumer layer not canonical store — Plan 18
- D-27: ChainScope dirty flag uses AtomicU64 generation counter not bool — generation counter detects reinforce() even if called-then-undone, which bool cannot — Plan 18
- D-28: golem-triage uses usearch crate (binary Hamming mode) for ANN — usearch supports binary vectors natively via Hamming metric; instant-distance is float-only and would require conversion — Plan 19
- D-29: Triage tier thresholds T0 < 0.2, T1 0.2–0.5, T2 > 0.5 — prd2/14-chain/02-triage.md routing table (Discard/Silent/ChainEvent/TriageAlert) is authoritative; CONTEXT.md's "T2 > 0.6" was approximate — Plan 19
- D-30: HDC item memory seed is 0xBAD0_BARD0_BARD0_B0 (from prd2/shared/hdc-fingerprints.md) — deterministic codebook across restarts and golem generations — Plan 19
- D-31: `Screen` trait gains `as_any_mut() -> &mut dyn Any` with `unimplemented!()` default — existing screens don't need updating; only screens that need channel draining implement it — Plan 20
- D-32: TriagedEvent fan-out uses `tokio::spawn` background task, not `broadcast` channel — broadcast has bounded capacity and drops events under load; background task forwards from single mpsc sender to multiple receivers — Plan 20
- D-33: If `TriagedEvent: Clone` is blocked (e.g., `HdcVector` non-Clone), substitute `Arc<TriagedEvent>` in the fan-out channel types — Plan 20
- D-34: Fluid protocol omitted from ProtocolRegistry (19 protocols, not 20) — Fluid not present in prd2 protocol catalog; if added later, implement SanctumProtocol and register — Plan 08d
- D-35: NremEngine is T0-only — no LLM calls during NREM; all replay is computational (HDC similarity + rule-based scoring); LLM reserved for REM/Evolution phases — Plan 22
- D-36: CounterfactualResult is a stub type in Plan 22 — full counterfactual generation belongs to Plan 16's ReflexionPipeline; NREM only holds a reference — Plan 22
- D-37: GrimoireWriter gains `update_strength(id: Uuid, delta: f32)` method — NREM replay needs targeted confidence updates without a full entry rewrite — Plan 22
- D-38: WorldBazaar is the canonical screen name for the WORLD-window bazaar view — SomaBazaar appeared in some prd2 sections but WORLD window owns it per prd2/02-terminal/windows.md — Plan 08b
- D-39: CounterfactualResult stub from Plan 22 (nrem/direction.rs) is replaced by CounterfactualScenario from Plan 23 — was always a placeholder; Plan 23 defines the full type with Pearl levels, Byrne controllability, and proposed guards — Plan 23
- D-40: RevmSimulatorTrait is a thin wrapper trait in rem/threats.rs; RevmSimulator gets a blanket impl — allows MockRevmSimulator in tests without a live chain; avoids depending on golem_chain directly from the trait boundary — Plan 23
- D-41: ThreatSimulator requires DreamTier::Hardened; Core tier returns Vec::new() silently — prd2/05-dreams/05-threats.md §Budget Tier Availability: T0 inference insufficient for detailed threat scenarios — Plan 23
- D-42: Adversarial mode threat outputs tagged ThreatProvenance::AdversarialDream and stored as ThreatAssessment records, never as executable strategies — prd2 safety constraint: line between imagining and planning an attack enforced at storage boundary — Plan 23
- D-43: Pheromone field query modeled as Option<Vec<ThreatScenario>> parameter into ThreatSimulator::run — actual pheromone client is Plan 40+; None is a valid production default until then — Plan 23
- D-44: golem-inference, golem-chain, golem-runtime, golem-daimon added as golem-dreams dependencies in Plan 23 — Plan 21 intentionally omitted them to keep scheduler lightweight; REM imagination requires LLM inference and chain simulation — Plan 23
- D-45: Uncovered threat staging confidence hardcoded to 0.30 (not configurable) — prd2/05-dreams/05-threats.md §Gap Analysis specifies 0.3 as a normative value grounded in the spec's confidence tier reasoning — Plan 23
- D-46: DreamJournal uses sled (not append-only file) — sled's tree iteration sufficient for daily-range queries at expected cycle volumes (1–3/day); avoids full scan on query_range() and stats() — Plan 24
- D-47: StyxSubmitter is a stub mpsc::Sender<StyxDreamPacket> in Plan 24 — Plan 40 wires the real WebSocket receiver; avoids circular dependency between golem-dreams and bardo-styx-client — Plan 24
- D-48: DepotentiationEngine requires two new Grimoire trait methods: `high_arousal_episodes()` on GrimoireReader and `update_pad_vector()` on GrimoireWriter — additive extension to Plan 12's traits; existing GrimoireStore tests must still pass — Plan 24
- D-49: DreamConsolidator enforces PLAYBOOK.md single-writer: only `apply_playbook_revisions()` calls `update_playbook()` — prd2/05-dreams/04-consolidation.md explicit constraint — Plan 24
- D-50: Web-sourced citations staged at confidence=0.45 (not 0.30); 14-day TTL vs 30-day for self-generated — retrieved facts have external grounding that self-generated hypotheses lack — Plan 24
- D-51: DreamJournalRecord.reasoning_trace stores Integration TEE thinking chain but is never fed back into subsequent inference — captures reasoning for owner review and successor inheritance only — Plan 24
- D-52: golem-hypnagogia is a separate crate, not a module inside golem-dreams — prd2/06-hypnagogia/00-overview.md says "extension to golem-dreams" but the scope (xenocognition, inner worlds, two PhaseRunners) warrants its own crate boundary — Plan 25
- D-53: DaliInterrupt.captured: bool, not Option<String> — raw fragment is always in the struct; captured signals whether HomuncularObserver approved it and it was written to Grimoire — Plan 25
- D-54: ScreenId gains DreamExplorer variant — continues D-16 pattern (ProtocolViews) without renumbering existing variants — Plan 25
- D-55: HypnagogicState::HypnagogicImagery has hauntological_intensity: f32 field — PerspectiveManager (Plan 08c) reads this to activate HauntologicalLayer without a separate event — Plan 25
- D-56: WorldGeometry::to_kluver_form() takes a compile-time dep on bardo_terminal::perspective::dream::KluverForm — if circular dep arises, extract KluverForm to golem-core — Plan 25
- D-57: DreamJournalRecord field names assumed from Plan 24 description; adapt TerminalDreamExplorerScreen if Plan 24 diverges — forward dependency acknowledged — Plan 25
- D-58: golem-tools replaces the stub ToolDef in golem_inference::types (Plan 11) — Plan 11 stub satisfied type references before golem-tools existed; Plans 27-35 import from golem_tools::ToolDef — Plan 26
- D-59: ToolContext.capability is Option<Capability<ToolDef>>, not Capability<SpecificTool> — executor receives params as serde_json::Value and dispatches dynamically; generic Capability<ToolDef> is consumed after the executor verifies it — Plan 26
- D-60: CircuitBreaker uses 10-request sliding window with 50% error threshold — matches prd2/07-tools/01-architecture.md; consistent with TypeScript opossum circuit breaker semantics — Plan 26
- D-61: ProviderPool max 100 connections, 5-minute idle TTL — balances connection reuse against stale provider handles after RPC endpoint rotation — Plan 26
- D-62: tools/mod.rs dispatch table uses match on &str, not trait objects — avoids vtable overhead on the hot path; each category plan appends arms to the match — Plan 26
- D-63: ALL_TOOL_DEFS starts as &[MOCK_TOOL_DEF] in Plan 26; populated by Plans 27-35 — prevents downstream plans from depending on an empty slice — Plan 26
- D-64: prd2/07-tools/02-tools-data.md defines exactly 9 data tools, not ~40 — the ~40 projection in planning notes was for the full category across all sub-plans; prd2 is authoritative — Plan 27
- D-65: resolve_chain helper shared across all 9 data tools lives in tools/data/mod.rs — accepts both chain name strings and numeric IDs; avoids per-tool duplication — Plan 27
- D-66: Subgraph-dependent fields (tvl_usd, volume_24h_usd, fee_apy_24h) return null on SubgraphClient failure rather than total error — partial on-chain data is more useful than a complete failure for data_get_pool_info — Plan 27
- D-67: Each tool module in data/ exports named statics (e.g. POOL_INFO_TOOL_DEF) rather than a single TOOL_DEF per file — allows pools.rs to own all 4 pool tools without splitting into thin single-tool files — Plan 27
- D-68: uniswap_check_allowance lives in quote.rs, not approval.rs — approval.rs is a stub for future write tools (approve_token, revoke_approval); allowance query logically pairs with quote tooling — Plan 28
- D-69: LP tools from prd2/07-tools/17-tools-uniswap-api.md deferred to Plan 29 — they carry category "lp" in the spec; mixing lp and trading tools exceeds the unit scope — Plan 28
- D-70: uniswap_api_create_swap_calldata runs both API-side simulation and an independent local Revm simulation — server and local simulation are independent safety layers; API simulation failure alone is insufficient — Plan 28
- D-71: Cross-chain Revm simulation covers only the source-chain lock tx — destination execution is off-process; the lock is the point of no return and the only locally simulable step — Plan 28
- D-72: Shared types (TickRange, TokenAmount, BatchOpResult, RebalanceEvent) declared in positions.rs and re-exported from lp/mod.rs — positions.rs is the first unit Codex writes; centralizing here avoids re-declaration across curve.rs, balancer.rs, managed.rs — Plan 29
- D-73: uniswap_api_migrate_liquidity has no SDK fallback — V2→V3 and V3→V4 atomic migration calldata generation has no equivalent in the TypeScript sidecar SDK; API is required; return CircuitOpen on API unavailability — Plan 29
- D-74: Trading API LP tools import TradingApiClient from trading::trading_api, never re-instantiate — shared rate bucket (3 req/sec) must be shared across all API tools within a session; re-instantiation would create independent buckets — Plan 29
- D-75: V4 fee collection uses zero-delta increaseLiquidity (amount0=0, amount1=0) — V4 PositionManager has no standalone collect(); this is the canonical pattern documented in prd2/07-tools/04-tools-lp.md §uniswap_lp_collect_fees — Plan 29
- D-76: Tick range arithmetic (price→tick→sqrtPriceX96) runs in-process using Rust U256 arithmetic, not via sidecar or on-chain call — TickMath is deterministic and stateless; computing on-chain adds latency and a potential call failure path with no benefit — Plan 29
- D-77: MakerDAO tools are Ethereum mainnet only (chain_id = 1) enforced at the tool level — Sky/MakerDAO Vat and CDP contracts do not exist on L2s; returning ChainNotSupported immediately avoids confusing downstream errors — Plan 31
- D-78: `lrt_request_withdrawal` is merged into `lrt_withdraw` — the prd2 spec defines lrt_withdraw to handle both instant and queued paths, distinguished by `eth_received: Option<String>` vs `request_id: Option<String>`; a separate request tool would duplicate the same logic — Plan 31
- D-79: `lrt_get_restaking_points` lives in `restaking/points.rs`, not `restaking/lrt.rs` — position data (balances, APY) and points accumulation (off-chain, speculative) are distinct concerns with different data sources; splitting the module keeps lrt.rs chain-focused — Plan 31
- D-80: EigenLayer withdrawal delay is read from `IDelegationManager.minWithdrawalDelayBlocks()` at call time, not hardcoded — the delay has changed via governance before; runtime read prevents stale constant bugs — Plan 31
- D-81: `lending_auto_deleverage` bypasses the phase gate via a named exception in ToolExecutor — preventing liquidation is a higher-order safety invariant than lifecycle phase restrictions; the bypass is explicit in executor code, not a silent override — Plan 31
- D-82: Health factor normalization formula `normalized = 1.0 - (1.0 / raw_hf)` used only in cross-protocol tools — per-protocol tools return raw HF to preserve precision; the normalized scale is a convenience layer for the lending dashboard — Plan 31
- D-83: AaveMarketState read falls back to direct Alloy IPool.getReserveData() when ProtocolStateEngine cache misses — the state engine (Plan 18) is an optional optimization, not a hard dependency; lending tools must be functional without it — Plan 31
- D-84: `lido_request_withdrawal` is registered as a separate ToolDef aliasing `lido_unstake`'s handler — the prd2 spec requires both names for semantic clarity; the handler is shared to avoid code duplication — Plan 31
- D-85: yield_ module name (not yield) — `yield` is a reserved Rust keyword — Plan 32
- D-86: `derivatives/types.rs` shared types module (Side, Greeks, OptionType, Moneyness, RiskLevel) — used across gmx.rs, panoptic.rs, cross_protocol.rs; avoids import cycles — Plan 32
- D-87: `pendle_buy_yt` phase gate is Thriving-only — YT decays to zero at maturity; speculation incompatible with non-peak lifecycle — Plan 32
- D-88: Convex tools are Ethereum mainnet only (chain_id = 1) — Convex contracts are mainnet-only per prd2 spec — Plan 32
- D-89: GMX liquidation price and PnL formulas included verbatim in Plan 32 Quick Reference — load-bearing for get_gmx_position.liquidation_price and unrealized_pnl_usd fields — Plan 32
- D-90: prd2/05-tools-bridge-aggregator.md targets Stargate/Across/Hop and 1inch/CoW/ParaSwap/Odos directly — task description mentioned LI.FI but canonical spec is authoritative; module named bridge/ as specified — Plan 33
- D-91: BridgeHttpClient is standalone, not reusing TradingApiClient — bridge/aggregator APIs use different auth schemes and base URLs; coupling to trading API key config would be wrong — Plan 33
- D-92: `aggregator_check_approval` uses Alloy direct on-chain allowance read, not an aggregator API call — on-chain is authoritative; avoids a round-trip for state that lives entirely on-chain — Plan 33
- D-93: Governance tools (veCRV, vlCVX, veAERO, veBAL) excluded from bridge tools — governance locks outlive Golems; locked tokens become permanently inaccessible at death — Plan 33
- D-94: Safety read-only tools use CapabilityTier::Read; write tools (batch_revoke, set_circuit_breaker, health alerts) use CapabilityTier::Write — read-only simulation provides no opportunity for fund loss regardless of LLM compromise — Plan 34
- D-95: All intelligence tools are CapabilityTier::Read — intelligence category is structurally unable to modify on-chain state — Plan 34
- D-96: Memory write tools (store_episode, manage_insight, consolidate, record_execution, import_grimoire) use CapabilityTier::Write — Grimoire writes affect belief state; capability gate prevents unconstrained external memory injection — Plan 34
- D-97: IL formula: IL = 2*sqrt(k)/(1+k) - 1 where k = price_new/price_current — verbatim from prd2/13-tools-intelligence.md for full-range V3 CPMM positions — Plan 34
- D-98: safety_batch_revoke_approvals is RiskTier::Layer1 (not Layer2/Layer3) — prd2 annotation "Layer1 (risk-reducing operation)"; revocation reduces attack surface — Plan 34
- D-99: MetaMask permission tools (metamask_*) live in tools/wallet/metamask/ sub-module — Category::Wallet per prd2/18-tools-metamask.md; session keys are wallet-layer delegation, not identity-layer — Plan 35
- D-100: ERC-8004 identity write tools are stubs pending Plan 45 — Plan 09 is read-only by D-12; Plan 45 implements writes; identity read tools (discover, lookup, reputation queries) are fully operational in Plan 35 — Plan 35
- D-101: ALL_TOOL_DEFS changes from &[MOCK_TOOL_DEF] (D-63) to OnceLock<Vec<ToolDef>> assembled at first call — avoids static lifetime issues with per-category Vec<ToolDef> returns while preserving single-source-of-truth — Plan 35
- D-102: stream_unsubscribe classified CapabilityTier::Read — prd2/19-tools-streaming.md: "classified as Read because it only modifies session state, not on-chain state" — Plan 35
- D-103: session_revoke_key classified RiskTier::Layer1 despite CapabilityTier::Write — prd2 annotation "Revocation is risk-reducing"; reduces blast radius — Plan 35
- D-104: Testnet tools guard against mainnet RPC URLs in the handler, not just the profile gate — misconfigured context could bypass profile checks; defense in depth — Plan 35
- D-105: CacheStack is cache-aside in handler.rs rather than a Tower middleware layer — Tower middleware requires boxing the full response body for write-through, adding complexity with no benefit at this scale — Plan 36
- D-106: HNSW index rebuilds every 100 inserts rather than per-insert — instant-distance has no incremental insert API; amortizing the rebuild keeps set() fast — Plan 36
- D-107: fastembed init failure degrades L2 silently rather than panicking — L3 and L1 still deliver meaningful savings; a missing model file should not prevent the gateway from starting — Plan 36
- D-108: L2 tests gated under `#[cfg(feature = "l2-tests")]` — nomic-embed-text-v1.5 ONNX weights (~130 MB) cannot be assumed present in standard CI — Plan 36
- D-109: ContentBlock::TextWithCache variant added to golem-inference in Plan 36 — L1 alignment requires expressing Anthropic's cache_control on content blocks; the type lives in the shared inference crate — Plan 36
- D-110: L3 uses moka TinyLFU rather than plain LRU — moka's admission policy handles bursty miss patterns better and the API is async-native — Plan 36
- D-111: ToolPruner reads ToolDef.embedding and ToolDef.token_cost fields added by Plan 37 — semantic selection requires pre-computed embeddings; computing at request time would add 100-500ms — Plan 37
- D-112: ToolPruner degrades to no-op (keeps all tools) when fastembed model is missing — a gateway that fails to start is worse than one that skips pruning — Plan 37
- D-113: Multi-step tool dispatch is a stub in Plan 37; real ToolExecutor (Plan 26) wired in Plan 41+ — tool execution requires ToolContext with live ChainProvider, which the gateway does not yet hold — Plan 37
- D-114: SessionStore uses moka::future::Cache with time-to-idle eviction — handles concurrent eviction correctly; Plan 39b can replace with persistent store — Plan 37
- D-115: KvCacheRouter prefix hash = SHA-256 of (system_prompt + sorted_tool_names) — SHA-256 is <1ms and exact; approximate prefix matching is not worth the complexity — Plan 37
- D-116: PII mask placeholder format `__CATEGORY_NN__` (double-underscore + category + counter) — double-underscore delimiters survive LLM reformatting better than `[WALLET_1]` and are easy to detect with `contains("__")` — Plan 38
- D-117: ONNX injection model loading is optional (None path = feature disabled, pattern check still runs) — ML injection detection is defense-in-depth; gateway must serve requests even if model files are absent — Plan 38
- D-118: BIP-39 post-validation on seed phrase regex matches — the 12-24 word regex has unacceptable false-positive rate on normal prose; wordlist validation cuts false positives to near-zero — Plan 38
- D-119: ONNX inference runs on rayon thread pool via spawn_blocking — ONNX Runtime is synchronous and CPU-bound; blocking the tokio executor would stall all concurrent requests — Plan 38
- D-120: Full response buffering before PII placeholder restore (no per-chunk streaming restore in Plan 38) — chunk-boundary splits of placeholders require stateful reassembly; deferred to a later plan — Plan 38
- D-121: PiiMasker created fresh per request (not shared) — forward/reverse maps are request-scoped; sharing across concurrent requests would cross-contaminate placeholder numbering — Plan 38
- D-122: ReputationTier defined as a stub enum in budget.rs in Plan 39a — Plan 46 (written) replaces this stub with a real on-chain lookup when implemented; see Plan 46's three-location replacement checklist — Plan 39a
- D-123: ProviderBackend is a separate trait from Plan 11's Provider — Plan 11's Provider uses AppError; ProviderBackend uses InferenceError from golem-inference for clean crate boundary — Plan 39a
- D-124: Rate limiting keyed on (agent_id OR wallet_address), not IP — agents are identified by identity; IP-based limiting breaks multi-agent deployments behind NAT — Plan 39a
- D-125: PaymentProcessor waits for 1 confirmation before retrying BlockRun — Base block time ~2s; risk of double-pay on retry outweighs the latency cost — Plan 39a
- D-126: SessionService uses HMAC-SHA256, not asymmetric JWT — sessions validated only by gateway itself; asymmetric keys add complexity with no trust-boundary benefit — Plan 39a
- D-127: BankrBackend delegates inference to BlockRun or OpenRouter and pays from the agent wallet — Bankr's role is economic (wallet-funded payment), not model provider — Plan 39a
- D-128: DeploymentMode variants named SelfHosted/Managed/Hybrid (not Embedded/Local/Remote per prd2) — operational topology clearer from the gateway operator's perspective — Plan 39b
- D-129: InferenceProfile lives in bardo_gateway::profiles not golem_inference::types — profile logic requires provider capabilities known only at gateway layer — Plan 39b
- D-130: StructuredOutputSchema wraps ResponseSchema + StructuredOutputMode together — avoids passing schema and mode as separate arguments through the pipeline — Plan 39b
- D-131: BatchProcessor eligible only for dream_* subsystems by default — dream cycles are the only latency-insensitive paths; heartbeat/risk/operator always use real-time inference — Plan 39b
- D-132: Think-tag detection runs on ALL backends in BardoStreamParser — DeepSeek R1/Qwen3 can arrive from Venice, BlockRun, or OpenRouter; coupling to a specific backend would miss tags — Plan 39b
- D-133: MultiModelOrchestrator::parallel() only for explicit cross-verification, not auto-applied — parallel calls are expensive; callers opt in for safety-critical paths only — Plan 39b
- D-134: SheafObserver lives in bardo-gateway not a separate bardo-sheaf crate — no callers outside the gateway process in Plans 36-39c; split if Plan 40+ Clade coordination causes circular dependency — Plan 39c
- D-135: MetricsCollector uses [Histogram<f64>; 8] fixed array for pipeline layers — layer count is fixed at 8; avoids heap allocation on the metrics hot path — Plan 39c
- D-136: GatewayMetrics is Clone via Arc<MetricsCollector> — Axum handler closures require Clone state; Arc prevents duplication — Plan 39c
- D-137: SpendAnalytics assembled from running counters at query time, not stored per-tenant — sufficient for single-operator gateway; persistent per-tenant store deferred until user management layer — Plan 39c
- D-138: SheafObserver lambda_ref starts at 1.0, calibrated to running 95th percentile via reservoir sample (cap 1000) — prevents contradiction alarms during bootstrap before calibration converges — Plan 39c
- D-139: emit_inference_event checks subscriber_count() before serializing — zero-cost when nothing is listening; matches EventFabric pattern from Plan 02 — Plan 39c
- D-140: Plan 43 follows the canonical creature PRD (`prd2/18-interfaces/28-creature-system.md` v4.12), not the older 7-layer archetype spec from `prd2/20-styx/05-tui-experience.md` — the older spec was superseded; Plan 08d stubs already used the dot-cloud architecture — Plan 43
- D-141: `golem-creature` exports `WireEvent` (not raw `GolemEvent`) — WireEvent is a creature-specific envelope that extracts only the creature-relevant fields from GolemEvent variants; decouples the rendering crate from the full event schema — Plan 43
- D-142: Death sequence is 16 seconds (6 stages), not 3 seconds — the 3-second figure in the task description comes from the older spec; the canonical PRD's 6-stage sequence is authoritative — Plan 43
- D-143: `SpriteDot` is defined in `golem_creature::sprite`, not re-exported from `bardo_terminal::widgets::spectre` — the creature crate owns its types; bardo-terminal re-exports after Plan 43 ships — Plan 43
- D-144: `SmallRng` seeded from the 32-byte sprite seed (not from entropy) — physics must be deterministic for a given Golem; SmallRng::from_seed([u8; 32]) is the correct API — Plan 43
- D-145: Dots sorted by `radius_factor` descending at generation time — cheap O(1) phase culling via slice truncation; recalculating sort order each frame at 60fps would waste 4800 sort ops/second for no benefit — Plan 43
- D-146: `AlmaState` (golem-daimon) is a dependency only for mood channels 10–12; PAD vector (channels 1–3) arrives via WireEvent from CorticalState — mood is the 4h EMA, not the per-tick PAD; the two have different sources — Plan 43
- D-147: `CreatureEngine::dissolve()` returns `DeathSequence` (an iterator) rather than mutating in place — callers (bardo-terminal) need to control playback timing and interleave with other animations; returning an iterator gives that control without coupling the engine to a frame clock — Plan 43
- D-148: `GolemBinaryInner` enum wraps typed states — `GolemBinary` is not generic over S — runtime branching (signal handlers, SSE push, admin commands) requires state inspection; making GolemBinary<S> generic propagates S through every async task holding a reference — Plan 41
- D-149: `GolemLifecycle<Active>.begin_dream()` returns `(GolemLifecycle<Dreaming>, HeartbeatEngine)` — engine returned by value — dreaming suspends the tick loop; returning the engine by value from begin_dream prevents "dream while ticking" at the type level; wake() re-accepts it — Plan 41
- D-150: `ProvisioningPipeline` step types defined in `golem-binary`, not `golem-runtime` — provisioning is an infrastructure concern (Fly.io, Privy, Permit2, Styx); golem-runtime must remain deployable standalone for self-hosted setups that handle provisioning externally — Plan 41
- D-151: `FundingOrchestrator::select_source` defaults to DirectTransfer when USDC balance is sufficient — custody mode (Delegation vs Embedded) is manifest-determined, not balance-detected; select_source is advisory for Embedded/LocalKey only; fund_wallet always calls delegation grant path in Delegation mode regardless — Plan 41
- D-152: `CaveatEnforcer` variants defined in `golem-binary/provisioning/steps.rs`, not `golem-chain` — the seven caveat types are funding-layer UX constructs that map to on-chain enforcers; the on-chain ABI lives in golem-chain; provisioning-specific enum avoids polluting the chain crate — Plan 41
- D-153: `GolemExtendedManifest` and `GolemCoreManifest` imported from `golem_core::config`, not re-defined in `golem-binary` — Plan 02 owns these types; no duplication — Plan 41
- D-154: StyxVault uses sled (not Qdrant or PostgreSQL) in Plan 40 — Plan 40 establishes type interfaces and integration points without introducing new managed service dependencies; Qdrant vector search is deferred (marked TODO) — Plan 40
- D-155: BloodstainRecord in bardo_styx::bloodstain is distinct from golem_mortality::thanatopsis::legacy::Bloodstain — Bloodstain is the golem-local struct for death event creation; BloodstainRecord is the Styx-server-side received payload; same semantic content, different lifecycle — Plan 40
- D-156: PheromoneField broadcasts PheromoneUpdate to all peers in the registry on deposit, not only clade members — Styx does not know domain-of-interest filters per peer in Plan 40; per-domain subscription filtering deferred to Plan 40+ — Plan 40
- D-157: StyxMessage::Authenticate must be the first message or the socket is closed — enforced in handle_socket() before registration; prevents resource exhaustion from unauthenticated connections — Plan 40
- D-158: LetheIndex uses a separate sled tree from StyxVault — different access patterns (Vault: per-golem-id range scan; Lethe: full-text search across all death entries); separate trees in the same sled::Db directory — Plan 40
- D-159: StyxSubmitter stub (Plan 24) is closed by golem-binary forwarding task in Plan 41, not inside Plan 40 — golem-binary owns the WS client connection to Styx; Plan 40 only defines the server-side handler — Plan 40
- D-160: ConsensusVote.weight defaults to peer.reputation at time of vote — reputation captured at vote-cast time, not re-read at resolution; avoids TOCTOU inconsistency if reputation changes mid-vote — Plan 40
- D-161: x402 payment verification is a TODO stub in Plan 40 — Plan 45 adds x402 middleware; Plan 40 uses API key authentication only — Plan 40
- D-162: DeathTestamentSections has nine fields matching prd2/06-thanatopsis.md S2 Step 5, not the five-category task brief summary — what_i_learned/what_i_got_wrong/what_confused_me/what_i_never_tested/what_killed_me/what_i_would_change/what_i_suspect/strongest_causal_edges/emotional_topology — Plan 42
- D-163: TeardownPipeline step 3 is backup_grimoire rather than sweep_wallets — wallet sweep is custody-mode-dependent and handled by the control plane after VM drain; backup_grimoire is the death-critical knowledge preservation step — Plan 42
- D-164: weismann_decay floors at 0.01 rather than 0.0 — zero confidence renders an entry completely useless; at 0.01 it is still retrievable and can be manually promoted; matches prd2 spec's `.max(0.01)` code example — Plan 42
- D-165: InheritanceApplier::apply does not enforce anti-proletarianization at ingest time — divergence check runs in SuccessionProtocol::check_divergence before the successor boots, not per-entry; ingest-time enforcement would require embedding every incoming entry — Plan 42
- D-166: ReplicantReport omits strategy_delta: StrategyDiff — StrategyDiff is not defined in any prerequisite plan; field omitted pending Plan 41+ or a future replication extension — Plan 42
- D-167: LifeReviewOutput.emotional_arc_narrative is a String rather than a structured type — Opus response for the narrative section is long-form prose; raw narrative stored alongside turning_points and narrative_arc; callers that need structure have the parsed fields — Plan 42
- D-168: compress_for_inheritance takes &GrimoireSnapshot rather than &dyn GrimoireWriter — compression happens at death, after the Grimoire has been snapshotted and is no longer being written; snapshot type avoids needing a live async connection — Plan 42
- D-169: CreatureWidget renders LayerSet (from Plan 43's 7-layer composition) rather than calling SpriteDot physics directly — Plan 44 is a pure consumer of Plan 43's output; separating rendering from physics engine allows the TUI to advance independently of engine internals — Plan 44
- D-170: GraveyardScreen uses ScreenId::FateGraveyard — matches Plan 04's ScreenId enum naming; semantically tied to the mortality system (Fate) rather than a generic graveyard concept — Plan 44
- D-171: BloodstainRecord stub declared in graveyard.rs rather than importing from bardo-styx — Plan 40 ships independently; importing pre-emptively would create a build dependency that blocks bardo-terminal until bardo-styx compiles — Plan 44
- D-172: AtmosphericPreset enum added to bardo_terminal::design::atmosphere in Plan 44 — Plan 08a defined AtmosphericStack but not a preset enum; preset-to-phase mapping is Plan 44's concern as it knows which atmospheric config corresponds to which lifecycle phase — Plan 44
- D-173: AppAction::Navigate(ScreenId) added to bardo_terminal::state in Plan 44 — Plan 04's AppAction lacked direct screen jumping; GraveyardScreen and PhaseOverlay require targeted navigation without sequential NextScreen cycling — Plan 44
- D-174: golem-identity is a new crate (not merged into golem-chain) — golem-chain is read-only by D-12; write operations introduce signer dependencies, fallback logic, and x402 advertising that would bloat the chain crate beyond its single responsibility — Plan 45
- D-175: Capability_ uses underscore suffix to avoid collision with golem_safety::capability::Capability<T> — the two types are conceptually distinct (skill declaration vs. unforgeable write token); renaming either would break existing plans — Plan 45
- D-176: metadata_cid Option<String> in RegistrationParams: None auto-generates a data: URI — agents without an IPFS node can still register; Tier 1 "IPFS Required" refers to rich metadata, not the bare CID field — Plan 45
- D-177: GolemIdentityId = [u8; 32] type alias (not a newtype struct) — bytes32 is the on-chain type; newtype would require Into/From boilerplate everywhere hex encoding is needed; downstream plans (46, 47, 51) import this alias directly — Plan 45
- D-178: IdentityRegistry::discover() does post-fetch min_score filtering rather than passing score to the contract — ERC-8004 registry does not expose a score-filtered query; client-side filtering is the only option — Plan 45
- D-179: X402Advertisement uses a separate EIP-712 TypeHash from IdentityClaim — x402 ads are published to HTTP endpoints, not submitted on-chain; separate TypeHash prevents replay of claim signatures as x402 ads — Plan 45
- D-180: submit_feedback rejects self-feedback at the Rust layer, not only at the contract — defense in depth; prd2 §4 owner-exclusion rules extend to feedback; catching it before a tx saves gas — Plan 45
- D-181: ReputationTier moved from bardo_gateway::budget stub to golem_identity::reputation canonical definition — reputation tiers are an identity/economy concept that belongs in the identity crate, not the gateway; gateway re-exports via pub use to preserve existing call sites — Plan 46
- D-182: Outcome weight is a raw multiplier in (0.0, 1.0], not a normalized audit score — the update formula uses weight as the auditor's stake weight; callers normalize audit scores to success_prob and pass weight separately via Success(w)/Failure(w) variants — Plan 46
- D-183: effective_weight uses sqrt(mean) rather than linear mean — sqrt compresses high-reputation influence, preventing winner-take-all dynamics where a Sovereign-tier agent with minimal stake dominates low-stake agents — Plan 46
- D-184: attest() is async and returns Result even in v1 stub — v2 will perform async on-chain writes; making the signature async now avoids a breaking change when the stub is replaced — Plan 46
- D-185: sample_count is NOT inherited in ReputationEngine::inherit() — inherited score represents lineage context metadata, not earned trust; successor must accumulate its own observation history to advance tiers — Plan 46
- D-186: Tier classification uses sample_count >= threshold (not >) — prd2 states "samples>=10" inclusive; off-by-one would prevent an agent with exactly 10 samples from reaching Verified — Plan 46
- D-187: CladeId derived from GolemIdentityId not wallet address — GolemIdentityId is the stable on-chain anchor; wallets can rotate — Plan 47
- D-188: CladeRegistry wraps Erc8004Registry — reuses moka cache from golem-chain — Plan 47
- D-189: Missing on-chain role/generation fields default to Member/0 rather than erroring — graceful degradation for pre-upgrade registries — Plan 47
- D-190: Reputation weighted mean uses generation-inverse × role weight — older founders with longer track records carry more signal — Plan 47
- D-191: Plan 46 must expose zero()/zero_accumulator()/add_weighted()/normalize() on ReputationScore — required by clade_reputation() — Plan 47
- D-192: CladeKnowledgePolicy enforced at Styx fan-out, stored per Grimoire entry — access control at write time — Plan 47
- D-193: Generational distance is absolute generation number diff, not tree depth — tree not fully reconstructible from on-chain data — Plan 47
- D-194: propagate_grief filter uses GolemIdentityId comparison; may need deceased_id explicit param if GolemId/GolemIdentityId types don't share a byte slice impl — Plan 47
- D-195: Apprentice grief modifier 0.5× is speculative; not in prd2 spec; awaiting spec clarification — Plan 47
- D-196: CladeNiche activity vectors hardcoded — niche semantics co-evolve with DeFi protocol catalog, same rate as code changes — Plan 47
- D-197: CladeMembership lacks niche field pending niche assignment pipeline — compute_carrying_capacity falls back to niches_occupied=1 — Plan 47
- D-198: MAX_CLADE=20 from prd2/01-golem/10-replication.md S4.1; MIN_CAPITAL=500 USDC from economy spec — Plan 47
- D-199: golem-economy uses 10% protocol_fee_rate for escrow-settled transactions; 5% micropayment rate from prd2 applies to x402-settled sub-$0.10 transactions only — Plan 48 implements escrow-settled paths; sub-$0.10 x402 micropayment paths are TODO pending x402 middleware from Plan 45 — Plan 48
- D-200: X402Payment imported from bardo_gateway::payments in handler signatures only; golem-economy does not depend on bardo-gateway at the crate level — avoids circular dependency (bardo-gateway depends on golem-inference which is in the same workspace) — Plan 48
- D-201: KnowledgeDemurrage in golem-economy is distinct from golem_mortality::KnowledgeDemurrage (Plan 13a) — Plan 13a owns Ebbinghaus decay used by the Curator; Plan 48 owns economy-facing demurrage (storage cost linkage, entry pricing impact) — Plan 48
- D-202: apply_demurrage(entry, seconds_elapsed) converts seconds to tick-intervals using tick_duration_secs=40.0 and validation_interval=250 ticks, matching prd2/02-mortality/05-knowledge-demurrage.md §5.1 defaults — Plan 48
- D-203: StyxMarketplace delegates CEK escrow to bardo-styx server-side; golem-economy does not implement AES-256-GCM or X25519 key wrapping — duplicating it would create two implementations of the same trust model — Plan 48
- D-204: type_weight constants calibrated from prd2 pricing ranges (LineageGrimoire $1–$25 vs StrategyFragment $0.10–$5.00 implies ~2.5x weight); prd2 does not give explicit numeric weights — Plan 48
- D-205: novelty_factor floor is 0.5 (not 0.05 from the alpha-decay price formula) — 0.5 floor prevents Grimoire entries from becoming nearly free after 30 days under the simpler freshness model — Plan 48
- D-206: ArchetypeCategory has 14 variants (10 initial TOML set + Lending/Staking/Derivatives/TechnicalAnalysis) — prd2 lists 14 categories with 42+ archetypes; all 14 category variants exist for forward compatibility even if initial TOMLs cover only 10 — Plan 49
- D-207: TOML files located at crates/golem-tools/archetypes/; runtime path resolved via GOLEM_ARCHETYPES_DIR env var, fallback to env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR") + "/archetypes" — avoids hardcoding absolute paths that break across machines and CI — Plan 49
- D-208: No new ToolProfile enum; resolve_profile() maps archetype tool_categories to the nearest existing ToolProfile variant from Plan 26 — ToolProfile already has 14 variants covering all needed combinations — Plan 49
- D-209: VaultAgentStrategy enum maps to vault-strategist's 10 research frameworks as 6 coarse variants — TOML ergonomics favor named strategies; Custom variant allows STRATEGY.md to specify framework parameters directly — Plan 49
- D-210: Plan 50 now uses GolemIdentityId from golem_identity::types (Plan 45) as author type — Plans 45-46 are written and must precede Plan 50; AgentIdentity (Plan 09) retained for pre-45 compatible fields only — Plan 50
- D-211: HermesSidecar socket path defaults to /tmp/hermes-golem.sock per prd2 spec; sidecar unavailability degrades gracefully (empty skill set, no error propagation) — Plan 50
- D-212: Extension trait (Plan 02) has no on_dream_onset or on_death hooks; both handled via on_after_turn (dream onset detected via AfterTurnCtx.is_dream_onset) and on_end (death detected via EndCtx.is_death) — same pattern as DaimonExtension — Plan 50
- D-213: No MCP server in L0; tools load in-process per prd2/19-agents-skills/08-mcp-integration.md; L1 MCP surface (skills/list, skills/invoke) is a stub in Plan 50; full HTTP MCP server wired in Plan 60+ — Plan 50
- D-214: No network port allocated for Hermes in Plan 50; L0 uses Unix domain socket; L1 MCP port TBD in Plan 60+ — Plan 50
- D-215: HermesEvent emitted via type-tag JSON pattern (EventPayload::Hermes { tag, payload: serde_json::Value }) to avoid circular dep between golem-core and golem-skills — Plan 50
- D-216: GolemIdentityId is a [u8; 32] stub in Plan 51 — Plan 45 defines the real type; Plan 51 uses the stub to avoid a forward dependency that blocks compilation — Plan 51
- D-217: ReputationScore and ReputationTier are stubs in Plan 51 — Plan 46 defines the real types; ReputationTier stub in Plan 39a (D-122) is the precedent — Plan 51
- D-218: coordination/ lives in golem-identity for now; extract to golem-coordination if circular dependency arises with Plan 45 identity writes — CONTEXT.md Crate Boundaries already anticipates golem-coordination as the long-term home — Plan 51
- D-219: ERC-1271 smart contract wallet verification is a stub (verify_erc1271 returns Ok always) — the real implementation requires an Alloy provider call against a live ERC-1271 contract; provider context not available until Plan 45 wires the identity registry — Plan 51
- D-220: IACPHook trait is a stub with no-op default implementations — hook wiring (reputation updates, x402 payments) requires Plan 45's x402 middleware; ERC-8183 hookable functions call the trait but no hooks are registered in Plan 51 — Plan 51
- D-221: OracleJudge outlier detection uses f64 standard deviation over raw answer bytes length, not semantic similarity — semantic outlier detection would require embedding inference; deterministic statistical outlier is sufficient for the bonding/slashing contract — Plan 51
- D-222: DelegationDag.max_depth is a struct field (u8) not a const — future plans may configure alternate depths for special agent archetypes; a const would prevent that without a breaking change — Plan 51
- D-223: validate_dag is synchronous — runs during ProvisioningPipeline<StepValidate> before any async I/O; sync keeps the validation path deterministic and eliminates cancellation edge cases during boot — Plan 51
- D-224: VeniceBackend stub from Plan 39a is replaced in-place (same module path bardo_gateway::providers::venice); the module file becomes a re-export shim pointing to bardo_gateway::venice::backend — existing import paths continue to compile without caller changes — Plan 53
- D-225: No explicit zero-retention opt-in header is sent to Venice — Venice's zero-retention is the default API contract for all requests, not an opt-in mode; no prd2 spec text describes a header; sending a fabricated header could be silently ignored or rejected — Plan 53
- D-226: HTTP 503 with X-Bardo-Error: private-inference-required is enforced in the Axum handler layer after SecurityClassRouter::enforce_routing(), not inside VeniceBackend — VeniceBackend does not have Axum context; the error type boundary is VeniceError which the handler maps to the HTTP response — Plan 53
- D-227: SecurityClass is propagated from SecurityClassRouter to ProviderRouter via InferenceRequest.metadata["security_class"] string field — avoids adding a new field to InferenceRequest (Plan 11 type) which would require a Plan 11 revision; metadata is already a serde_json::Value map — Plan 53
- D-228: Dream DIEM budget has no pay-per-request fallback — dreams are always private (prd2 §3: "dreams always run" even when waking budget is exhausted); falling back to a retaining provider would violate the zero-retention guarantee for dream content — Plan 53
- D-229: DiemTracker.end_of_day() is triggered by a tokio::time::interval task at midnight UTC — cron-style scheduling is not available within bardo-gateway; tokio interval is the standard async timer; UTC midnight matches Venice's daily DIEM reset cycle — Plan 53
- D-230: SecurityClassRouter reads DataClassification::CryptoSensitive from PiiMasker output (Plan 38) to trigger OwnerPii without re-running PII detection — PiiMasker already ran in SafetyLayer before the router; double-detection would be redundant and slower; DataClassification is the PiiMasker's output type — Plan 53
- D-231: bardo-gateway adds golem-economy as a crate dependency in Plan 54 — the dependency chain is bardo-gateway → golem-economy; golem-economy does not depend on bardo-gateway (D-200 established this); no cycle — Plan 54
- D-232: AutoReplenishPolicy thresholds are named constants (DEFAULT_REPLENISH_THRESHOLD_CREDITS, DEFAULT_REPLENISH_AMOUNT_USDC, DEFAULT_MAX_REPLENISH_PER_DAY) — hardcoded numerals in the policy struct fields are untraceable across future plans; named constants make deviation intentional — Plan 54
- D-233: BankrBackend functions without MetabolicLoopMonitor by defaulting to ThrottlePolicy::FullAccess — prevents a monitor initialization failure from making the entire inference stack unavailable; the Golem can still earn and spend, just without metabolic throttling — Plan 54
- D-234: SustainabilityRatio uses total_daily_inference_cost as the denominator, not total_daily_cost (which would include gas and compute) — prd2 §03-bankr.md task description specifies the formula as total_daily_revenue / total_daily_inference_cost; gas and compute costs affect the broader AgentEconomy sustainability in Plan 48 but not this ratio — Plan 54
- D-235: ThrottlePolicy::from_ratio produces ReducedThroughput without requiring step-by-step traversal through FullAccess — the ratio can drop multiple points between monitor ticks; computing terminal state from raw ratio is correct and avoids stale intermediate states — Plan 54
- D-236: VERIFICATION_STAKE_THRESHOLD_USD (500.0) and VERIFICATION_AGREEMENT_MIN (0.7) are module-level constants, not SelfFundingConfig fields — these are safety parameters from prd2 §2 (cross-model verification), not operator-configurable; making them configurable would allow operators to bypass the safety property — Plan 54
- D-237: VaultFeeConverter uses micro-USDC (6-decimal integer) for replenish_amount_usdc, matching X402Payment.amount_usdc units — consistent with bardo-gateway payment types from Plan 39a; avoids f64 precision loss in payment amounts — Plan 54
- D-238: AgentCashClient::claim_payment is called in a background tokio::spawn after PaywallHandler delivers content — claim failure should not roll back an already-delivered payment; the background retry pattern matches bardo-gateway's payment retry in Plan 39a (D-125) — Plan 55
- D-239: MortalityClock trait gains extend_by_revenue(amount_usd: f64) -> Result<()> as an additive method with default no-op — RevenueLifespanExtender requires it; default no-op preserves backward compatibility for Plan 13a implementors that don't yet override it — Plan 55
- D-240: DiscoveryPublisher.build_advertisement() stubs eip712_signature as [0u8; 65] — signing key is not available at the time the publisher is constructed (Plan 41's StepIdentity runs during provisioning); real signature wired when Plan 45's ClaimSigner is integrated — Plan 55
- D-241: PaywallHandler GET /v1/knowledge/:entry_id always returns 402 regardless of buyer identity — x402 protocol requires 402 on every unpaid GET; buyers who already hold a valid payment proof use POST directly — Plan 55
- D-242: RatingAggregator::weighted_mean truncates to shorter slice on mismatch rather than erroring — mismatched slice lengths indicate a caller bug, not a protocol error; truncation gives a meaningful result from valid pairs and lets the caller surface the mismatch through their own logging — Plan 55
- D-243: UNISWAPX_REACTOR_ADDRESS = Exclusive Dutch Order Reactor v2 — canonical UniswapX intent reactor; TODO comment for address verification since Codex has no internet — Plan 57
- D-244: IntentSigner takes &LocalSigner<k256::SigningKey> not Box<dyn Wallet> — alloy sign_typed_data requires concrete type; can generalize to T: Signer if Plan 56 defines a Wallet trait — Plan 57
- D-245: daily_rent_usdc and self_assessed_value are u64 USDC (6-decimal) — consistent with Plan 48 economy layer pricing; avoids float rounding on accumulated rent — Plan 57
- D-246: AmAmmBidder::compute_bid returns Option<BidParams> — zero expected fees means missing data; bidding blind wastes capital — Plan 57
- D-247: BehavioralPhase match arms use Thriving/Stable/Conservation/Declining/Terminal from prd2 — if Plan 02 uses different variant names, Codex must adjust; comment added at match site — Plan 57
- D-248: max_range_half_width is a price fraction (0.02 = ±2%), not ticks — tick conversion is pool-specific; caller converts before submitting to execute_uniswap_action — Plan 57
- D-249: EmotionFeeModulator uses only arousal and dominance from PadVector — pleasure is not a fee-relevant signal per prd2 §4 — Plan 57
- D-250: FeeModulation preserves base_fee_tier alongside recommended_tier — enables emotion_attribution tracking in LpDecisionEpisode — Plan 57
- D-251: MutationApplier::apply is synchronous — validates only; caller executes on-chain change via Plan 56's ActionPermit pipeline — Plan 57
- D-252: build_proposal sets confidence=0.6 as base for promoted mutations — shadow validations increment this; 3 validations → PlaybookStatus::Active per Plan 56 — Plan 57
- D-253: golem-integrations is a new crate rather than modules added to golem-chain or golem-identity — extension bounties have external API dependencies (Slice, Self Protocol, Status) that should not pollute the minimal-dependency chain/identity crates; golem-integrations can be excluded from a deployment without affecting core operation — Plan 58a
- D-254: VaultSubgraphClient wraps golem_tools::SubgraphClient rather than reimplementing GraphQL transport — Plan 26 already provides a production-quality paginated GraphQL client with TTL cache; duplicating it would create two HTTP client pools for the same service — Plan 58a
- D-255: SelfProofVerifier nullifier cache is in-memory (RwLock<HashSet>) not persistent — nullifiers are per-session; the production deployment notes that a persistent registry (Redis or sled) is required for long-running Golem processes; the in-memory version is correct for testing and short-lived sessions — Plan 58a
- D-256: Status Network chain_id 1952959480 is the current Status Network L2 value; this should be read from ChainRegistry if/when Status Network is added as a supported chain in Plan 09 — Plan 58a
- D-257: Slice attestation cert is stored as raw DER bytes, not parsed into an x509 struct — x509 parsing adds a dependency (x509-parser or rcgen); DER bytes are sufficient for API round-trips; callers that need cert fields can parse on demand — Plan 58a
- D-258: ENS offchain resolution uses CCIP-Read (EIP-3668) for Golem subdomains, not per-Golem on-chain registration — per-Golem on-chain ENS registration costs gas for every new Golem; CCIP-Read delegates to the Bardo API which maintains the name→address mapping — Plan 58a
- D-259: CeloVaultStrategy panics at construction if target_allocations don't sum to 1.0 — this is a programmer error (misconfigured strategy), not a recoverable runtime error; a strategy with allocations that don't sum to 1.0 is meaningless — Plan 58b
- D-260: MentoOracleClient reads SortedOracles median directly rather than using Mento Exchange contract — SortedOracles is the canonical price feed; Exchange introduces swap paths that can deviate from spot under stress; oracle reads are for risk management, not execution — Plan 58b
- D-261: DeathArtGenerator failure is non-fatal to the death protocol — death must complete regardless of SuperRare API availability; GolemTombstone is the primary death record; the NFT is a decorative output — Plan 58b
- D-262: SuperRareClient image generation delegates to an external inference endpoint (Venice or SD) rather than implementing a local generative model — image generation requires GPU resources not available on the Golem VM; the inference endpoint is pluggable — Plan 58b
- D-263: LifetimeArtParams::from_tombstone is a pure function with no async — GolemTombstone contains all necessary data; no chain reads required at death time for art parameter extraction — Plan 58b
- D-264: Arkhai contract addresses are placeholder stubs — Arkhai has not yet deployed to Base mainnet; addresses must be updated before production use; tests mock the provider rather than relying on specific addresses — Plan 58b
- D-265: ArkhaiEscrowClient wraps Plan 51's JobEscrow type rather than defining a new escrow type — JobEscrow is the ERC-8183 standard; ArkhaiEscrowClient is an on-chain implementation of that standard; using the same type maintains compatibility with Plan 48's CommerceBazaar — Plan 58b
- D-266: FundingRateArbitrageEngine::annualize_8h multiplier is 1095 (365 × 3) — three 8-hour funding periods per day; this is the standard perp annualization convention — Plan 58b
- D-267: LocusTaskClient stub always returns NotImplemented rather than a no-op success — callers must detect Locus unavailability and fall back to internal scheduling; a no-op success would silently swallow tasks — Plan 58b
- D-268: LocusTask.coordination_policy uses a different enum than golem_identity::coordination::composition::CompositionType even though they have overlapping variants — LocusTask models external orchestration scheduling; CompositionType models internal agent composition; they evolve independently — Plan 58b
- D-269: golem-mortality is the only new dependency added by Plan 58b (for GolemTombstone in SuperRare) — Celo and bond.credit use alloy directly; Arkhai re-uses Plan 51 types already in golem-identity; Locus is self-contained — Plan 58b
- D-270: LocalKey panics in production rather than returning an error — a panic is unconditional; an error return would allow callers to catch it and start anyway; a misconfigured production deployment must fail immediately and loudly at startup, not silently after the Golem has been running for hours — Plan 60
- D-271: Three custody lanes (hot/cold/fail-safe) rather than two — hot and cold alone leave an irrecoverable scenario where the owner loses wallet access and the session key expires simultaneously; the fail-safe lane is the on-chain-verifiable escape hatch; the two-lane model would require relying on Privy or a custom recovery mechanism — Plan 60
- D-272: requires_cold_lane is a free function not a method on PrivilegeModel — routing decisions must be made before PrivilegeModel is fully constructed (e.g. during config validation); a free function allows calling with just action and value without holding a custody reference — Plan 60
- D-273: FailSafeLane::recovery_address is immutable post-provisioning — if recovery_address were changeable via cold lane, a cold lane compromise lets an attacker redirect recovery to themselves; recovery address must be set once at provisioning and never changed through any in-process mechanism — Plan 60
- D-274: ApiKeyStore is in-memory (DashMap) not a database — O(tens) of API keys per deployment; DashMap lookup at ~100ns per request is orders of magnitude faster than a DB round-trip; key additions require a gateway restart (hot-reload deferred) — Plan 60
- D-275: JWT TTL is 24h; TUI session tokens stored in ~/.bardo/auth.json (0o600) are treated like SSH keys and get the same 24h lifetime — JWT bearer tokens appear in logs, proxy buffers, curl history; 24h bounds the exposure window; TUI re-authenticates silently — Plan 60
- D-276: RbacPolicy is a static match table not a database-backed policy engine — 4 roles × 4 permissions = 16 combinations; auditable in 20 lines of code; OPA/Casbin would add operational complexity for no gain at this scale — Plan 60
- D-277: SessionStore is in-memory not Redis — v1 single-instance decision; sessions drop on gateway restart which is acceptable; interface is narrow (create/validate/invalidate) so Redis backend can be swapped in when horizontal scaling is needed — Plan 60
- D-278: Finite-trace LTL (LTLf semantics) not full model checking — Golem trace is finite and bounded by session lifetime; full LTL is defined over infinite words; three-valued output (Satisfied/Violated/Inconclusive) avoids Buchi automaton construction; O(n) per property — Plan 61
- D-279: 23 templates from Trace2Inv not arbitrary LTL formulas — covers specific failure modes from PRD post-mortems; achieves 74.1% neutralization rate; prevents operators from writing unsound formulas; templates instantiated from GolemConfig at runtime — Plan 61
- D-280: WitnessDag uses B256 tx hashes not UUIDs — B256 is independently verifiable on-chain via any archive node; UUIDs are internal identifiers that cannot be verified against on-chain state; tamper-evidence requires anchoring to external facts — Plan 61
- D-281: CounterExample stores full GolemState slices — states may evict from trace ring buffer before post-mortem analysis; counterexamples capture the violating window at detection time; storage cost is acceptable because violations are rare — Plan 61
- D-282: ViolationReporter flags not blocks (emergency_stop_on_violation defaults off) — temporal violations may be on liveness properties still being satisfied; blocking on Inconclusive or Violated liveness is counterproductive; PolicyCage has veto authority; temporal monitors have escalation authority — Plan 61
- D-283: VerificationPipeline runs every 10 ticks not every tick — O(23 × 1000) predicate evaluations per run; decoupled from per-tick path to keep REFLECT step budget predictable; default 10 ticks = ~20 minutes at theta=120s; configurable to 1 for aggressive monitoring — Plan 61
- D-284: WitnessDag in golem-safety not golem-chain — safety/audit artifact not chain-interaction; records Golem's cognitive trace for accountability; does not read/write on-chain state; adjacent to AuditChain from Plan 10 — Plan 61
- D-285: golem-uniswap depends on golem-safety for MevProtectionLayer; if a build cycle arises, MevProtectionLayer moves to golem_uniswap::mev::layer — CONTEXT.md uses golem_safety::mev as the canonical home — Plan 62
- D-286: AddLiquidity routes Direct (no private mempool) — LP provision does not change pool spot price in a predictable directional way; a front-runner cannot profit by copying an add-liquidity tx; private routing overhead is unjustified — Plan 62
- D-287: Sandwich + Swap routes to UniswapX intent (not Flashbots) when pair is supported — UniswapX intents are filled by off-chain fillers who compete on price, eliminating the public mempool entirely; Flashbots still has a timing window between bundle submission and block building — Plan 62
- D-288: $5,000 threshold for forced private routing — below $5,000 expected MEV extraction ($2.50–$7.50) is comparable to routing latency cost; above $5,000 the dollar loss from a sandwich ($25+) materially exceeds routing overhead — Plan 62
- D-289: Default swap routing is MEV Blocker not Flashbots — MEV Blocker's 90% back-run rebate to users is a net positive for smaller swaps; Flashbots Protect has no rebate; for the default case the rebate outweighs Flashbots' marginal inclusion speed advantage — Plan 62
- D-290: Commit-reveal is opt-in not required — requiring it for every action doubles block latency (commit block N, reveal block N+1) and adds on-chain gas for the commitment tx; latency penalty is harmful for time-sensitive rebalances; meaningful only when private RPC and UniswapX are both unavailable — Plan 62
- D-291: SandwichDetector uses heuristic match (same pool, same direction, size threshold) not profit simulation — full revm sandwich simulation adds 50–200ms latency and requires pool reserve data that may not be in cache; heuristic has acceptable false-positive rates for routing decisions — Plan 62
- D-292: MevProtectionLayer::apply is synchronous — only calls SandwichDetector::scan and MevRouter::route, both pure CPU work over an already-fetched mempool snapshot; caller is responsible for async mempool fetch before calling apply — Plan 62
- D-293: pool_address: Option<Address> added to ActionPreview in Plan 56 — required for SandwichDetector to know which pool to scan; None for action types that don't target a single pool; this is an additive change to Plan 56's struct — Plan 62
- D-294: OnboardingWizard implements Screen so the wizard is a single ScreenId::Onboarding entry in ScreenRegistry; internal step navigation is invisible to App — Plan 63
- D-295: WalletFundingScreen threshold is $50 (5,000,000 in 6-decimal USDC) — prd2/13-runtime/07-onboarding.md specifies $50 minimum as sufficient for 30-day Golem operation; below $50 the Golem would hit Terminal phase within hours of first boot — Plan 63
- D-296: PortalWizardPartial.custody is String not CustodyMode — bardo-gateway must not depend on golem-safety; the custody string is validated against a whitelist in the API handler when written — Plan 63
- D-297: TUI wizard has 5 steps while portal wizard has 7 — portal includes Warden deployment and ERC-8004 registration as explicit steps because they require on-chain transactions from the web frontend; TUI defers both to ProvisioningPipeline (Plan 41) which handles them during summoning — Plan 63
- D-298: CapabilityReport.detect() is synchronous despite checking network — uses TcpStream with 2-second timeout; blocking for 2s on first render is acceptable for the very first screen; async would require wiring tokio runtime into render() which contradicts the TUI's synchronous render model — Plan 63
- D-299: PortalWizardPartial.warden_delays keys are String not an action category enum — full action category type lives in golem-tools (Plan 26); bardo-gateway does not depend on golem-tools; handler validates keys against a static whitelist on receipt — Plan 63
- D-300: AuthSetupScreen connection test uses reqwest::blocking — user explicitly triggers the test via keypress; 5-second block is visible (spinner) and expected; adding a tokio channel and background task for a single user-triggered HTTP call would double the code for no benefit — Plan 63
- D-301: WalletFundingScreen polling runs at 10-second intervals — USDC deposits settle in ~2s on Base L2 but polling faster burns RPC quota with no UX benefit — Plan 63
- D-302: EventBus uses broadcast not MPSC — N concurrent WS/SSE subscribers each need independent delivery; MPSC is single-consumer — Plan 63b
- D-303: broadcast capacity 1024 — ~20 min buffer at volatile-market event rate; historical replay delegated to EventFabric ring buffer — Plan 63b
- D-304: CheckpointManager keeps last 3 files — covers write-crash / corruption / bad-state rollback without unbounded disk growth — Plan 63b
- D-305: /metrics endpoint is unauthenticated — Prometheus scraper standard; security boundary is IP allowlist at network layer — Plan 63b
- D-306: SSE and WebSocket both supported — WS for full-duplex Portal; SSE for proxy-friendly read-only consumers — Plan 63b
- D-307: GolemEventEnvelope is gateway-local not re-exported GolemEvent — decouples HTTP wire format from golem-core internal variant changes — Plan 63b
- D-308: session_keys_encrypted in PersistentState never plaintext — secp256k1 spending keys must not appear in backup archives — Plan 63b
- D-309: min_vitality_change filter suppresses micro-updates — VitalityUpdate fires every tick; dashboards need threshold-gated delivery — Plan 63b
- D-310: ReferralCode uses 8-char alphanumeric not UUID — typeable/shareable verbally; 41 bits entropy sufficient at realistic redemption rates — Plan 65
- D-311: LeaderboardCalculator::compute is stateless batch recompute not incremental — incremental update requires write lock per vitality tick across all opted-in agents; 60s batch recompute is cheaper and simpler — Plan 65
- D-312: Challenge wager is USDC not XP — XP is non-transferable progression currency; real financial stakes require a market-clearing unit; 10% protocol fee from Plan 48 RevenueSplit applies — Plan 65
- D-313: GolemSnapshot in golem-engagement is a separate type from GolemState (golem-runtime) — avoids heavyweight compile-time dependency from golem-engagement on golem-runtime; decouples social layer from cognitive internals — Plan 65
- D-314: InactivityDetector fires only at cadence boundaries (±1 day tolerance) — prevents prompt flooding when callers poll frequently — Plan 65
- D-315: UxError::Internal never writes debug string to response body — prevents information disclosure of internal paths, type names, and provider URLs — Plan 65
- D-316: request_id_layer echoes existing X-Request-Id from client — enables Portal/TUI/CLI to assign their own correlation IDs and trace them through the full gateway path — Plan 65
- D-317: Kelly fraction capped at max_single_position_pct from RiskProfile — full Kelly assumes perfect parameter estimates and infinite horizon; a Golem with noisy estimates (high estimation_var) or short lifespan (low remaining_ticks) would overbet without the cap; converts theoretical optimality into practical safety — Plan 59
- D-318: SOMATIC_ALARM_THRESHOLD = 0.75 is a module-level const, not a RiskProfile field — making it configurable would allow operators to disable the safety property by setting it to 1.0; only AvoidTrade and SeekSafety BehavioralBias variants trigger the gate — Plan 59
- D-319: WardenHandle uses wall-clock time (tokio::sleep) not block numbers for delay enforcement — Base/L2 block times vary 0.5s–4s+ under congestion; wall-clock delay reliably gives 30 seconds to cancel regardless of network conditions; block-number delays could deliver zero or hundreds of seconds of actual wall-clock time — Plan 59
