- D-320: PolicyCage::load_registry_from_chain is a stub returning Default in Plan 59; real Alloy IPolicyCage calls deferred to Plan 60 — requires live ChainProvider with known contract address per deployment; interface defined now so Plan 60 can implement without changing callers — Plan 59
- D-321: GolemRiskEngine::assess is async but Layers 1–3 and 5 are synchronous in Plan 59 — Layer 4 (DeFi threats: oracle verification, MEV protection) requires async chain reads added in Plans 60+; async signature avoids a breaking change when async work lands in assess() — Plan 59
- D-322: DrawdownMonitor high-water mark update requires explicit update_nav() call, not passive observation — passive HWM tracking would require the monitor to hold an Arc<portfolio_state> and poll; explicit update_nav() makes the call site explicit in the heartbeat tick pipeline, matching prd2's spec that HWM updates are owner-signed on-chain — Plan 59
- D-323: BayesianConfidence uses Beta(2,2) prior not Beta(1,1) uniform — Beta(2,2) is mildly uncertain and centered at 0.5, matching prd2's "mildly uncertain" language; Beta(1,1) would give equal mass at every confidence level including 0 and 1, making early failures or successes outsized before real observations — Plan 59
- D-324: ActionGate warden delay doubling (2× at risk_score > 0.7) uses fixed multiplier not configurable — 0.7 threshold and 2× multiplier derive from prd2/10-safety/06-adaptive-risk.md's "multi-layer advisory accumulation" threshold; configurable multiplier would be set to 1× by operators trying to speed up trading, defeating the purpose — Plan 59
- D-355: RevisionId is a 12-variant enum, not a string tag — compile-time exhaustiveness prevents misspelling in config — Plan 69
- D-356: RevisionApplier lives in golem-grimoire, not golem-eval — revisions touch Grimoire internals; golem-eval depends on golem-grimoire, not the reverse — Plan 69
- D-357: HybridRetriever wraps GrimoireReader::retrieve() rather than replacing it — existing call sites keep four-factor behavior; opt-in to hybrid by using HybridRetriever — Plan 69
- D-358: BM25 uses SQLite FTS5 (already in golem-grimoire's rusqlite bundled build), not a separate crate — zero additional infrastructure — Plan 69
- D-359: EmbeddingDriftDetector runs during DreamPhase::Evolution, not as a separate background task — reuses dream budget window; aligns with prd2 "weekly, during Loop 3 or Dream Integration" — Plan 69
- D-360: Drift reference corpus of 20 docs selected at Grimoire initialization, stored in drift_reference table — random sampling each week would measure noise not drift; fixed corpus gives meaningful week-over-week comparison — Plan 69
- D-361: QualityGate::evaluate is sync — all gate metrics are pre-computed; no async I/O required at gate evaluation time — Plan 69
- D-362: EvaluationLifecycle state stored in SQLite in golem-eval (not Grimoire) — lifecycle transitions are operational metadata, not cognitive knowledge — Plan 69
- D-363: Artifact quality specificity dimension uses regex, not LLM — $0.00, deterministic; LLM reserved for future_utility in AdmissionGate — Plan 69
- D-364: RegimeTag defined in golem-grimoire (not golem-core) — it is a retrieval primitive (regime_match factor in HybridWeights); consumers import from golem-grimoire — Plan 69
- D-365: EmbeddingMetadata added to GrimoireEntry as Option<EmbeddingMetadata> — existing entries without metadata still function; drift detection skips None entries — Plan 69
- D-366: GrimoireWriter gains update_strength() and update_last_accessed() — reuses D-37 update_strength() from Plan 22; update_last_accessed() is new for Ebbinghaus retention formula — Plan 69
- D-367: CognitiveQualityDashboard pulls quality distribution from KnowledgeQualityReport (Plan 68a) rather than recomputing — avoids duplicating evaluation logic — Plan 69
- D-370: DataBridge trait uses tokio::sync::watch not broadcast — screens need only the latest value, not every intermediate update; watch guarantees the last written value with no missed-event semantics — Plan 70a
- D-371: DataHandles groups all 11 receivers in one struct rather than returning them individually — screens destructure what they need; no hidden dependencies from partial DataHandles — Plan 70a
- D-372: LiveDataSource spawns 8 independent background tasks rather than one polling loop — allows domain-appropriate update rates (per-frame CorticalState vs 10s Grimoire) without a master poll cycle — Plan 70a
- D-373: MockDataSource uses a deterministic seed, not random — allows snapshot test reproducibility; test fixtures can assert exact pixel-for-pixel output — Plan 70a
- D-374: GrimoireStats is a terminal-specific type in bardo_terminal::data_bridge, not re-exported from golem-grimoire — grimoire's public API is storage-oriented; the stats summary is a display-layer concern — Plan 70a
- D-375: HeartbeatFsmStage is a separate enum from golem-heartbeat's internal FSM type — decouples TUI from heartbeat internals; stage names chosen for readability on screen — Plan 70a
- D-376: CognitionScreenState stores signal_history as [VecDeque<f32>; 32] in screen state, not in DataHandles — history accumulates in the UI layer only; golem-heartbeat does not produce historical arrays — Plan 70a
- D-377: PredictionsScreenState category_accuracies is [f32; 4], not [f32; 16] — screen shows only the four headline domains; full 16-slot array from CorticalState is available via borrow but not all rendered — Plan 70a
- D-378: MockDataSource phase cycles every 500 ticks rather than being fixed — demonstrates phase transition rendering without requiring a real dying golem — Plan 70a
- D-379: ActivityScreenState scroll_offset and search_query are in screen state, not DataHandles — filter state is UI-local; the underlying ActivityLog is shared — Plan 70a
- D-380: SettingsScreen saves via golem-runtime config API, not direct file write — avoids bypassing config validation; the API rejects unknown keys — Plan 70a
- D-381: HomeScreen shows last 5 DecisionCycleRecords in recent_ticks, not 1 — single tick is too volatile; 5-tick rolling view gives stable behavioral context — Plan 70a
- D-382: DataBridge trait is object-safe (no generic methods) — allows Arc<dyn DataBridge> storage in AppState without Box<dyn DataBridge + Send + Sync> gymnastics — Plan 70a
- D-383: InsightsScreenState top_insights limit is 10, not configurable — screen vertical space fits exactly 10 rows at standard terminal heights; more rows require scroll which breaks the at-a-glance design intent — Plan 70a
- D-400: FATE/COMMAND screen modules are flat files, not subdirectories — each screen fits in one file; subdirectory indirection adds no value at this scale — Plan 70c
- D-401: DebugPanel owned directly by App, not behind Arc<RwLock<_>> — only the main render loop writes and reads it; no concurrent access — Plan 70c
- D-402: Debug event log subscription created only when debug_mode == true — avoids wasting a broadcast slot and paying serialization cost in production — Plan 70c
- D-403: StateDump triggers GolemState serialization on [r] keypress, not continuously — serialization is ~10ms; continuous background update would visibly impact frame budget — Plan 70c
- D-404: SystemScreen.binary_hash computed once at boot via sha2::Sha256 over the executable — static integrity anchor; identical on every call after boot — Plan 70c
- D-405: PositionsScreen IL estimate uses full-range CPMM formula — narrow-range formula requires tick math not always in cache at render time; full-range is a conservative approximation — Plan 70c
- D-406: StrategiesScreenState.available_in_phase computed in background task, not render function — BehavioralPhase changes are infrequent; prevents repeated match in 60fps render loop — Plan 70c
- D-407: LegacyScreen reads SuccessorSeed only if already prepared; does not trigger succession — side effects from render paths are forbidden by TUI architecture — Plan 70c
- D-408: ConfigScreen reads GolemConfig on screen focus, not continuously — config is quasi-static; per-frame polling would allocate a large struct at 60fps — Plan 70c
- D-409: DebugPanel HeartbeatProfiler sources timing from DecisionCycleRecord.phase_latencies — no separate profiling instrumentation needed — Plan 70c
- D-445: DeathMaskPipeline failure is non-fatal to ThanatopsisEngine Phase III — the death testament and Styx upload are the canonical records; the NFT is decorative output; a SuperRare API outage must not prevent a Golem from completing its death protocol — Plan 73
- D-446: composite_dream_hash is keccak256 over sorted journal entry IDs, not image content — image content is not deterministic across model versions; the hash provides provenance over the Golem's dream archive — Plan 73
- D-447: High pleasure → lower reserve price (generous pricing, up to 20% discount) — pleasure in PAD is felt wellbeing; a Golem at peace with its death has less need to maximize extraction; capped at 20% to prevent trivially low reserves — Plan 73
- D-448: AuctionType threshold is dominance > 0.5 (not >=) — dominance exactly at 0.5 defaults to Dutch; the Golem did not die in clear control — Plan 73
- D-449: auction_duration_hours floor is 1.0 hour — a zero-duration auction is meaningless; even the most aroused death deserves at least one hour for bidders — Plan 73
- D-450: SelfAppraiser::default_mode maps narrative arc strings not NarrativeArc enum — avoids an import cycle between golem-oneirography and golem-mortality — Plan 73
- D-451: Narcissus fallback is $1.00 not $0.00 — a zero reserve blocks the auction mechanics downstream; $1.00 is the minimum meaningful price — Plan 73
- D-452: golem-oneirography adds golem-mortality and golem-economy as Cargo dependencies in Plan 73 — both were deferred in Plan 72 because the death mask types were not yet defined — Plan 73
- D-325: golem-eval is a library crate in crates/ not a tests/ binary — multiple integration test crates import GauntletRunner and EvalScenario; library crate allows re-export without duplication; ImmortalBaseline would cause circular dev-dep if placed inside golem-mortality tests — Plan 68a
- D-326: Bootstrap seed pinned to 0xBAD0_BARD0_BARD0_B0 — same constant used for HdcTxEncoder (Plan 19) and CampaignConfig default; deterministic codebook across restarts; CI bounds must be identical across runs with same seed — Plan 68a
- D-327: RegimeTag is defined in golem-eval not golem-core — RegimeTag is purely an evaluation-time label; production runtime uses BehavioralPhase for agent state; mixing these would import eval infrastructure into the production runtime; CONTEXT.md's MarketRegime in bardo_gateway::cache::config (Plan 36) is a separate enum with different semantics (cache TTL tuning) — Plan 68a
- D-328: ImmortalBaseline wraps ImmortalControl rather than redefining it — ImmortalControl (Plan 13b) is the canonical production shadow-clock type; evaluation layer adds campaign metadata without duplicating the mortality logic; field names in from_mortal_config() are noted as assumptions to be verified against Plan 02's GolemConfig schema — Plan 68a
- D-329: effect_size returns EvalError::InsufficientData rather than NaN on empty input — NaN propagation would silently corrupt campaign summaries; explicit error forces callers to handle missing immortal control data — Plan 68a
- D-330: AblationConfig uses 6 bool fields rather than an enum set — individual booleans allow runtime composition (disable economic + stochastic simultaneously without predefined variants); EffectMagnitude enum is a separate reporting concern — Plan 68a
- D-331: GauntletRunner.run_scenario uses todo!() — wiring the actual golem runtime requires all agent subsystems live and plumbed; the type skeleton compiles; Plan 68b (or the campaign wiring plan) fills in the agent instantiation; this is the intentional stub boundary — Plan 68a
- D-332: CalibrationCurve uses fixed 10 buckets (0.05, 0.15, ..., 0.95) — ECE computation is standard; 10 buckets matches the reliability diagram convention in Guo et al. ICML 2017; implementer fills in bucket construction logic — Plan 68a
- D-340: FastLoopRunner uses subprocess invocation rather than an in-process test harness — cargo-nextest requires clean process isolation for accurate timing and parallel test slotting; embedding it in the eval binary would share address space with the system under test, defeating the isolation requirement — Plan 68b
- D-341: CampaignConfig::n_runs_per_regime defaults to 100 not 10 — the thesis validation spec requires 10 runs per config for factorial analysis; simulation campaigns are shorter-horizon and need more samples to detect smaller effects (Cohen's d >= 0.3 requires ~89 samples per arm at 80% power, alpha=0.05) — Plan 68b
- D-342: AbTest uses Welch's t-test not Student's t-test — Welch's does not assume equal variances between variants, which is the correct assumption when comparing different GolemConfig fragments that may produce very different outcome distributions — Plan 68b
- D-343: EpistemicAutopsy reads DeathTestament directly rather than re-querying the Grimoire — at autopsy time the golem is dead and its Grimoire may have been compressed into a Genome (Plan 42); the Testament is the authoritative post-mortem record and is always available even after Genome compression — Plan 68b
- D-344: SuccessorPrescription wraps rather than replaces SuccessorRecommendation (Plan 42) — the existing type captures the golem's own self-assessment; SuccessorPrescription adds the eval system's objective assessment from external analysis of the testament — Plan 68b
- D-345: KlDivergenceCalibration uses empirical quantile binning not equal-width binning — KL divergence distributions are heavily right-skewed; equal-width bins would put 90%+ of events in the lowest bin and make calibration analysis uninformative — Plan 68b
- D-346: WatchBloomFprReport measures FPR at three canonical sizes not just the current operational size — filter capacity changes when ChainScope rebuilds; understanding FPR across the capacity curve prevents surprises when the interest list grows after a regime change with many new counterparties — Plan 68b
- D-347: AdwinSensitivity sweeps delta from 0.001 to 0.1 — below 0.001 ADWIN is so conservative it misses fast regime changes; above 0.1 the false alarm rate rises above 5% per 1000 blocks, flooding the triage pipeline with spurious regime-change signals — Plan 68b
- D-348: DecayCorrectnessReport uses relative error not absolute error — interest scores span [0.0, 1.0] and initial values vary by entry; absolute error would make high-score entries appear to have larger deviations even when the decay rate is correct — Plan 68b
- D-385: EventsScreen ring buffer cap is 50 (not 100 like ChainEventFeedScreen) — curiosity-scored events are a small fraction of raw chain events; 50 fills the screen without redundancy — Plan 70b
- D-386: VitalityUpdate carries precomputed ticks_remaining: Option<u64> — mortality runtime has full context for projections; terminal should not re-derive from raw clock state — Plan 70b
- D-387: Stochastic clock displays ∞ when projected_hours is None — Gompertz-Makeham hazard at high vitality is below float resolution; ∞ is accurate and preferable to a misleading finite number — Plan 70b
- D-388: PAD bars use centered zero-line rendering, not left-anchored — PAD range is [-1.0, +1.0]; a center-anchored bar communicates signed value correctly — Plan 70b
- D-389: WriterGroup signal-to-group mapping is a compile-time constant table — CorticalState field assignments are stable; runtime-configurable mapping would add maintenance cost without benefit — Plan 70b
- D-390: RegimeBadge defined locally in markets.rs, independent of bardo_gateway::cache::config::MarketRegime — gateway MarketRegime is a cache TTL hint; terminal RegimeBadge is a display type with colors and confidence; they evolve independently — Plan 70b
- D-391: ProtocolsScreen receives pre-computed ProtocolsSnapshot, not a direct ProtocolStateEngine handle — snapshotting top 50 entries on a 5-second background task is cheaper than re-scanning DashMap at 60 FPS — Plan 70b
- D-392: StorageLayer enum defined locally in protocols.rs — terminal display abstraction may not map 1:1 to engine-internal tier terminology — Plan 70b
- D-393: MarketsUpdate.price_feeds derived from ProtocolStateEngine UniswapV3 pool prices for MVP — dedicated Chainlink/Pyth integration deferred to a future plan — Plan 70b
- D-415: golem-eval is a library crate, not a test binary — importable by CI tooling and monitoring scripts — Plan 71
- D-416: Safety invariants H5/H6 use max_violations: Some(0), not statistical thresholds — a single approved over-limit trade is a hard bug — Plan 71
- D-417: FullLoopScenario wiring bodies use todo!() — correct wiring depends on all Plans 01–70c being complete; type interfaces are locked — Plan 71
- D-418: HeartbeatTickT0 < 100ms and MemorySearch < 25ms at p95 are verbatim from prd2/appendices/performance-targets.md — Plan 71
- D-419: ThesisVerdict uses PascalCase enum variants matching Rust idiom for the prd2 TypeScript string values — Plan 71
- D-420: InProcessHarness included unconditionally, not feature-gated — avoids coordination coupling with Plans 68a/68b — Plan 71
- D-421: cohen_d/benjamini_hochberg/gt_score implemented in-crate to avoid heavy statistics crate dependency — Plan 71
- D-422: GateVerdict::PassWithCaveats distinct from Pass — operators need to see non-blocking failures — Plan 71
- D-423: DailyCostUnderTarget uses max_violations not statistics — $5/day is a hard operational budget — Plan 71
- D-424: PboPassesGate is a meta-hypothesis on methodology validity, not on agent performance — Plan 71
- D-460: ArtOutput holds prompt + style parameters, not pixel data — Golem VMs have no GPU; pixel generation delegates to external image endpoints — Plan 74
- D-461: ArtGenerator::generate is synchronous — pure CPU arithmetic; caller wraps in spawn_blocking if needed — Plan 74
- D-462: WitnessDagSnapshot is a portable serializable copy not a reference to WitnessDag — WitnessDag is non-Clone; snapshot avoids long-lived borrow across async boundary — Plan 74
- D-463: CrucibleArtworkGenerator gates on risk_score > 0.7 matching Plan 59 warden doubling threshold — operational definition of high-stakes decision in Bardo system — Plan 74
- D-464: GalleryStore flush uses serde_json on full Vec<GalleryEntry> — gallery size is bounded by rarity of art events; binary encoding not needed — Plan 74
- D-465: ImageRenderer for Kitty/iTerm2 writes directly to stdout, not into ratatui Buffer — Buffer cannot carry terminal-specific escape sequences; spaces mark cells to prevent differential renderer overwrite — Plan 74
- D-466: render_ansi_blocks stub does not decode base64 PNG/JPEG — image crate adds 40+ transitive deps; colored placeholder rendered instead; full decoding deferred to future plan behind feature flag — Plan 74
- D-467: ScreenId::Gallery added without renumbering existing variants — consistent with D-16, D-54 patterns — Plan 74
- D-468: format_unix_timestamp uses arithmetic approximation not chrono — avoids chrono dep if not already present — Plan 74
- D-469: GalleryView::cols computed by caller not renderer — decouples layout from rendering; App::handle_resize must update cols — Plan 74
- D-470: HauntologicalDiptychGenerator sorts dream pair chronologically by start_tick internally — enforces left=earlier/right=later convention; callers need not pre-sort — Plan 74
- D-475: `BscHypervector` is a newtype over `Box<[u64; 160]>` rather than reusing `HdcVector` from `golem_core::hdc` directly — `HdcVector` in Plan 02 is described as a stub for 10,240-bit BSC; `golem-ta` owns the full implementation with explicit algebra operations; the newtype makes operations type-safe and prevents accidental mixing with Plan 19's `HdcVector`s which use a different seed namespace — Plan 75a
- D-476: `PatternAlgebra::bind` uses `rustfft` circular convolution rather than XOR — the plan spec explicitly calls for "frequency domain multiplication" for bind; XOR is the Plan 19 triage encoder's bind operation (which is bit-level, not frequency-domain); these are different HDC algebras serving different purposes; the TA crate's bind encodes temporal order information that XOR cannot — Plan 75a
- D-477: `HdcEncoder` seed is `0xBAD0_BARD0_BARD0_TA01` not `0xBAD0_BARD0_BARD0_B0` — the two encoders (triage vs TA) must have orthogonal codebooks; sharing a seed would make triage transaction hypervectors accidentally similar to TA market state hypervectors; the `TA01` suffix makes the relationship explicit — Plan 75a
- D-478: `TaCorticalExtension` uses `_pad: [u8; 32]` to reach 64 bytes — the 8 f32 fields consume 32 bytes; the alignment attribute `#[repr(C, align(64))]` requires the struct to be exactly 64 bytes for safe array-of-structs usage; explicit padding makes the layout contract clear rather than relying on implicit alignment padding — Plan 75a
- D-479: `momentum_zscore` is unbounded f32 stored in the struct despite the spec saying "clamped at ±3.0 for display" — the struct stores the raw computed value; display clamping is the caller's responsibility; storing the clamped value would discard information at the boundary and make the raw signal unavailable for downstream calculations — Plan 75a
- D-480: `TaCorticalExtensionWriter` uses `Arc<Mutex<TaCorticalExtension>>` not `crossbeam::atomic::AtomicCell` — `AtomicCell<TaCorticalExtension>` requires T: Copy, and `TaCorticalExtension` with padding bytes is technically Copy but the semantics are subtle; `Mutex` is correct and the lock contention is negligible (one writer per Gamma tick, one reader per heartbeat tick) — Plan 75a
- D-481: `TemporalConvolution` uses `VecDeque<BscHypervector>` not a ring buffer of raw bit arrays — `VecDeque` gives O(1) push/pop; the per-entry cost is one `Box<[u64; 160]>` = 1,280 bytes; at window_depth=20 this is 25.6 KB total, well within acceptable per-crate memory budget — Plan 75a
- D-482: `PatternLibrary` is capped at 256 entries — linear scan over 256 × 160-u64 arrays for cosine similarity is approximately 256 × 160 × 8 = 328 KB of memory reads; at modern L2 bandwidth (~100 GB/s) this completes in well under 1 µs; beyond 256 entries a proper ANN index (like Plan 19's `CuriosityAnnIndex`) would be needed — Plan 75a
- D-483: `TaFeelingsBinder` is a unit struct with a single static method rather than a trait — no runtime polymorphism is needed; the binding rules are a fixed policy from the plan spec; making it a trait would add indirection with no current caller using a different implementation — Plan 75a
- D-484: `CrossProtocolBinder` stores `prev_composite: Option<BscHypervector>` to compute divergence — the divergence signal is the change from the last tick's composite to the current one; the writer owns the binder and calls it once per Gamma tick, so `prev_composite` is always the correct reference frame — Plan 75a
- D-485: `TaCorticalExtensionWriter` emits `GolemEvent` only when Δ > 0.05 on any signal — emitting an event every Gamma tick (~250 per hour at delta=14s) would flood the `EventFabric` ring buffer; the 0.05 threshold is configurable in `TaConfig`; zero-drift markets (all signals stable) produce zero events, which is correct — Plan 75a
- D-490: PredictionOutcome adds predicted_value: f64 alongside actual_value — required by CalibrationMetrics::from_outcomes to populate reliability curve (predicted, actual) bin pairs — Plan 75b
- D-491: PredictionDomain is #[repr(u8)] — prevents silent index drift if variants are reordered; misaligned as_index() would corrupt category_accuracies writes without a compile error — Plan 75b
- D-492: PredictionEngine.outcome_history capped at 2000 — ~5 days at one prediction/tick; tail entries have negligible EMA weight; prevents unbounded memory growth — Plan 75b
- D-493: Accuracy EMA alpha = 0.10 matches EpistemicClock rolling window — both track prediction quality; divergent smoothing would produce inconsistent accuracy signals from the same events — Plan 75b
- D-494: TA-prediction bridge is caller-side in heartbeat ANALYZE, not inside PredictionEngine — engine does not depend on TaCorticalExtension; bridge logic lives where both CorticalState TA fields and LLM outputs are available in the same tick — Plan 75b
- D-495: PredictionEngine is not an Extension trait impl — predictions need explicit control over when outcomes are observed (only at resolution_time) and when generated (only at T1/T2); Extension hooks would conflate the two — Plan 75b
- D-496: Surprise threshold is 0.49, not 0.25 — restricts Grimoire writes to genuine bad misses (worse than random), preventing spam during normal market noise — Plan 75b
- D-497: golem_runtime::prediction re-exports from golem-ta — preserves Plan 16 call sites without a separate migration plan; PredictionStore alias keeps existing API surface intact — Plan 75b
- D-505: RiemannianManifold uses adaptive grid from observations not a fixed lattice — 4D lattice at resolution 20 would have 20^4 = 160,000 nodes; O(N²) Dijkstra over 160K nodes is too slow for 100-tick updates; adaptive grid from pool observations (~5–50 nodes) keeps Dijkstra under 1ms — Plan 76a
- D-506: Ricci curvature estimated via local variance deviation, not Christoffel symbols — exact Christoffel symbol computation requires smooth functional form for g_ij(x); with a constant metric tensor, all Christoffel symbols are identically zero (flat manifold); the variance-deviation proxy captures regime curvature from data without assuming a smooth metric field — Plan 76a
- D-507: pool_tvl in StatePoint uses raw liquidity as proxy — Plan 18 D-26 explicitly forbids derived USD values in canonical protocol state; liquidity (u128) is the closest available proxy; users needing USD TVL must add a separate Subgraph query — Plan 76a
- D-508: sqrtPriceX96 converted to f64 via to_string().parse() — U256 does not impl Into<f64>; the to_string route preserves full precision for the integer part; for sqrtPriceX96 values up to ~2^128, f64 loses precision below ~2^75 but the price ratio (/ 2^96)^2 remains meaningful — Plan 76a
- D-509: UniswapV4Pool returns None from protocol_state_to_point — V4 pool state fields not confirmed in CONTEXT.md at Plan 76a time; adding a None arm is safer than guessing field names; implement when V4 state layout is confirmed in Plan 75a or 78a — Plan 76a
- D-510: LiquidityFlowAnalyzer::collect_state_points calls engine.all_states() — ProtocolStateEngine's exact query API depends on Plan 18 implementation; if `all_states()` does not exist, use `iter()` over the hot DashMap or `snapshot()` if available; adapt field name at implementation time — Plan 76a
- D-511: update_interval_ticks default is 100 — matches the prompt.md spec note "Update interval: every 100 ticks"; at theta=120s per tick this is ~200 minutes; at a faster tick rate (e.g. gamma=2s), 100 ticks = 3.3 minutes — plan spec is authoritative, not tick rate assumptions — Plan 76a
- D-512: Geodesic K=6 nearest neighbors — balances graph connectivity against O(N × K) edge count; at N=50 nodes, 6 neighbors gives 300 edges total; too few neighbors (K<3) causes disconnected graphs; too many (K>15) adds negligible accuracy — Plan 76a
- D-515: BscHypervector::cosine_similarity assumed present from Plan 75a; if API differs, HebbianLearner co-activation calls must be updated — Plan 76b
- D-516: initialize_equal_shares called after every birth; O(n) acceptable given n ≤ 50 — Plan 76b
- D-517: eviction candidates marked Dead in-place, pruned on next 10-tick sweep rather than removed immediately — Plan 76b
- D-518: apply_forgetting increments age counter before reinforcement resets it; callers must call apply_prediction_outcomes before tick() — Plan 76b
- D-519: population_share floor 0.001 prevents replicator extinction trap for recovering signals — Plan 76b
- D-520: diversity_index is unnormalized Shannon entropy; H=0 monopoly, H=ln(50)≈3.9 perfect diversity — Plan 76b
- D-521: PredictionDomain from Plan 16 reused to keep fitness feedback loop closed without translation layer — Plan 76b
- D-522: SignalMetabolismEngine is fully synchronous; all operations O(n) n≤50, no IO — Plan 76b
- D-523: EXPLORATION_FRACTION=0.20 is conceptual guide, not a hard birth gate; birth is blocked only at total max_signals cap — Plan 76b
- D-524: prune_dead uses retain(); Dead signals persist up to 10 ticks; find_index callers must check is_dead() before acting — Plan 76b
- D-525: TimeSeriesMatrix uses ring-buffer shift (not VecDeque) — PC algorithm needs contiguous row slices; VecDeque copy-on-read adds O(N×T) allocation per independence test — Plan 76c
- D-526: PcAlgorithm::max_cond_set_size defaults to 3 not N-2 — full PC is O(N^k) independence tests; k=3 covers sparse DeFi graphs and completes in < 200ms per run at N=10, T=500 — Plan 76c
- D-527: Fisher Z-test not KCI/HSIC — HSIC is O(N²T²); Fisher Z is O(T) per pair; sufficient for linear-Gaussian regime of short-window DeFi price series — Plan 76c
- D-528: LCG noise in do_calculus not rand crate — avoids heavy rand dependency; LCG is sufficient for i.i.d. ancestral sampling; deterministic per intervention.node seed aids debugging — Plan 76c
- D-529: extract_outcome_from_diff stubs gas_used — real slot extraction requires AbiRegistry (Plan 17) mapping DefiVariable → storage slot per protocol; Plan 77+ replaces the stub — Plan 76c
- D-530: MirageCounterfactualRunner forks sequentially — parallel spawning requires connection pool over MirageConfig's single URL; callers needing parallelism spawn multiple runners — Plan 76c
- D-531: WitnessDag::iter_paths is a soft dependency — Plan 61 defines verify_path() but not a path iterator; reconcile_with_witness() returns empty Vec if iter_paths() is not available — Plan 76c
- D-532: CausalGraph::parents_of excludes undirected edges via symmetry check — undirected CPDAG edges represent unidentified directions; including them as parents would introduce spurious do-calculus adjustments — Plan 76c
- D-533: No GPU acceleration — target is Fly.io VM without GPU; serial CPU PC at N=10, T=500 completes in < 200ms, within Gamma tick budget — Plan 76c
- D-535: PointCloud defined in golem_ta::tda::types rather than reusing Plan 76a's type — independent evolution; becomes a type alias if Plan 76a exports a compatible type — Plan 77a
- D-536: Dimension-2 cutoff for Rips complex construction — H₂ not meaningful for 2D market point clouds; O(N⁴+) cost for higher dims is prohibitive at N=200 — Plan 77a
- D-537: H₁ complexity bound at 50,000 simplices returns empty barcode not panic — bound almost never hit in practice; empty barcode is valid and produces a flat landscape — Plan 77a
- D-538: L² distance uses max(H₀_dist, H₁_dist) not Euclidean combination — avoids cross-scale domination without requiring scale calibration — Plan 77a
- D-539: regime_stability uses rational decay 1/(1+d/threshold) not logistic sigmoid — avoids exp() overflow, threshold-parameterized, monotonically decreasing — Plan 77a
- D-540: TopologyRegimeSignal.regime_label is &'static str not String — fixed match on two integers; heap allocation per tick for a display label is wasteful — Plan 77a
- D-541: update_interval defaults to 50 ticks per spec; override via DetectorConfig — Plan 77a
- D-542: Persistence landscape grid resolution defaults to 100 not 1000 — sufficient fidelity for L² regime detection; 10x cost for negligible accuracy gain — Plan 77a
- D-543: Historical reference is pointwise mean of last 10 landscapes not Fréchet mean — Fréchet mean requires iterative optimization; pointwise mean is O(history×grid) and unbiased for stationary barcodes — Plan 77a
- D-545: PatternAuctioneer uses proportional VCG allocation — proportional allocation preserves bid magnitude information; step auctions lose relative confidence levels — Plan 77b
- D-546: Clarke tax payments are informational only — VCG payments track shadow cost for future analysis; no USDC transfer at auction time — Plan 77b
- D-547: 20% exploration budget conceptually reserved but allocated inline — avoids a two-queue design; Plan 78+ can promote nascent-specific routing if needed — Plan 77b
- D-548: signals_conflict() uses domain equality as conflict proxy — PatternSignal from Plan 75a lacks an explicit prediction_direction field; domain-equality proxy avoids Plan 75a schema changes — Plan 77b
- D-549: dream_pass uses Bernoulli trial per pattern — equivalent to Poisson for small libraries; simpler implementation; effective rate = base_rate × 10_240 matches spec intent — Plan 77b
- D-550: Inheritance discount applied once at import — re-applying per generation hop would collapse fitness exponentially across multi-generation lineages beyond spec intent — Plan 77b
- D-551: breed() uses HDC XOR not majority-vote bundle — XOR maximizes offspring distance from parents; bundle produces near-copies; diversity is the point — Plan 77b
- D-552: PatternGene holds BscHypervector by value — genes are portable cross-process artifacts; copying 1,280 bytes per gene is acceptable at death/import frequency — Plan 77b
- D-559: DefiIndicatorSuite samples at 30-tick cadence to balance responsiveness vs computation cost — 30 ticks = ~7.5s at 4 ticks/sec; shorter windows react to noise; longer windows miss MEV spikes — Plan 77c
- D-560: IlVelocityIndicator returns dIL/dt in units of 1bp/tick — allows Golem to rank position risk by revenue impact — Plan 77c
- D-561: ConcentratedLiquidityAsymmetryIndicator uses only position slots within ±3 ticks of current spot price — tail slots decay in influence; avoids stale-data drift — Plan 77c
- D-562: MevPressureGradient encoded as log₁₀(pressure) clamped [0, 2] — compresses long tail of MEV estimates; preserves relative urgency — Plan 77c
- D-563: OracleDivergenceIndicator flags > 2% deviation (conservative vs 5% threshold in golem-uniswap) — TA layer signals early; execution layer has stricter gate — Plan 77c
- D-564: All 10 indicators normalized to [0, 1] range by their own design constraints; no rescaling in suite — callers apply confidence weights based on regime; normalization preserves signal variance structure — Plan 77c
- D-565: golem-ta does not depend on golem-uniswap for SandwichSignal — AdversarialType::SandwichAttack carries its own semantic meaning; coupling would invert the crate dependency graph (TA depending on the execution layer) — Plan 78a
- D-566: NodeId hex literals in canonical_delta_for_type are symbolic pseudocode — Plan 75a PatternLibrary defines real NodeId values; Codex replaces at implementation time — Plan 78a
- D-567: RedTeamDreamer::generate_scenarios uses cycle-seed round-robin not ImaginationOutput — REM integration stub; fuller wiring from REmEngine::SyntheticScenario deferred until Plan 21-24 consolidation is stable — Plan 78a
- D-568: Tukey IRLS initial estimate is median(xs) not zero — median is within 1-2 MAD of the final solution; starting from zero diverges on asymmetric distributions — Plan 78a
- D-569: SOFTENING_THRESHOLD (0.6) < MANIPULATION_SIMILARITY_THRESHOLD (0.8) — graduated response: softening at 0.6, hard alert at 0.8; equal thresholds would collapse to a step function — Plan 78a
- D-570: AttackLibrary evicts oldest non-seed FIFO — O(1) scan; preserves most recent attack variants; seeds never evicted — Plan 78a
- D-575: `pattern_vec` stored as `[u64; 160]` not `HdcVector` (Plan 02's 10240-bit stub) — `HdcVector` in golem-core was a stub pending the TA track; Plan 75a defines `BscHypervector` as the concrete 160-word type; this plan uses the concrete type directly — Plan 78b
- D-576: LanceDB `pattern_vec` column uses `Binary(1280)` not a float vector column — Hamming distance over bits requires raw byte access; LanceDB's float ANN index (IVF-PQ, HNSW) is semantically incorrect for binary HDC vectors — Plan 78b
- D-577: AVX2 path uses scalar `u64::count_ones()` within 4-word loop bodies rather than VPSHUFB-based vectorized popcount — VPSHUFB-based Harley-Seal implementation requires 20+ intrinsic calls and careful alignment; the scalar fallback within the AVX2 path is ~2× slower than full Harley-Seal but still fits the sub-100ns target on warm L3 cache; the note in the code directs Codex to consult Mula et al. (2017) if the performance target is missed in benchmarks — Plan 78b
- D-578: `BINDING_SIGMA_THRESHOLD = 2.0` is a `pub const` not a configurable field on `SomaticTaIntegration` — the 2σ threshold is the spec's stated value; making it configurable would allow operators to lower it to 0, storing every outcome; the spec's intent is "significant outcomes only" — Plan 78b
- D-579: `SomaticTaExtension` registered at `layer() = 3` (same layer as `DaimonExtension`) — both are affect-layer extensions; ordering within layer 3 is controlled by registration order in ExtensionRegistry (Plan 02's topological sort); `SomaticTaExtension` should be registered AFTER `RuntimeDaimon` so the PAD bus is written before this extension reads it — Plan 78b
- D-580: `SomaticContext` does not implement `Serialize` — it contains `TaSignal` references that may not be serializable, and it is only needed for the current tick's GATE step; it is not persisted; LanceDB stores `PatternSomaticBinding` instead — Plan 78b
- D-581: `EmotionalSignalModulator::modulate` returns unity weights when `alarm_level > 0.75` even though the suppression path is upstream in the GATE step — defense in depth; if the GATE step's alarm check is skipped for any reason, the modulator still does not amplify suppressed-pattern signals — Plan 78b
- D-582: `PnlRollingStats` uses population standard deviation (divides by N, not N-1) — sample stddev with N-1 has higher variance at small N; with capacity=200 and minimum 10 observations, population stddev is the more stable estimator for threshold comparison — Plan 78b
- D-585: PhiEstimator uses greedy bipartition (local optimum) not exhaustive search — 2^9-2=510 bipartitions is feasible but the greedy approximation runs in O(n^2) vs O(2^n); greedy gives a lower bound on true Phi, which is sufficient for the integration_threshold gate; exact Phi is NP-hard for n > 12 and not needed here — Plan 78c
- D-586: φ uses minimum information partition (MIP) criterion: track minimum phi across explored bipartitions, not the bipartition with highest phi — IIT's canonical definition requires the bipartition that minimizes integrated information; selecting maximum would give an upper bound, not the true phi — Plan 78c
- D-587: TaDecision injected into DecisionCycleRecord.extensions as serde_json::Value — avoids adding a hard golem-ta dependency to golem-heartbeat; loosely coupled via JSON key "ta_decision"; ActionGate deserializes on demand — Plan 78c
- D-588: SessionAggregator α=0.01 (half-life ≈ 69 ticks) rather than α=0.05 (half-life ≈ 14 ticks) — session scale should smooth over ~100 ticks to distinguish from tick-level noise; α=0.05 is too responsive — Plan 78c
- D-589: BayesianWeightUpdater uses Dirichlet prior [5,3,2] not uniform — tick scale is the primary TA signal; lifetime scale accumulates slowly and should start with low confidence weight; prior encodes this structural belief rather than starting from symmetric ignorance — Plan 78c
- D-590: TaSystemCalibrator softmax temperature T=2.0 not T=1.0 — T=1.0 would aggressively suppress low-accuracy subsystems after only a few wrong predictions; T=2.0 keeps a smoother weight distribution during early learning before calibration data accumulates — Plan 78c
- D-591: MIN_WEIGHT=0.01 per subsystem — prevents complete subsystem suppression; even a 1% weight preserves the subsystem's monitoring role; a weight of 0 means the subsystem never contributes to calibration updates — Plan 78c
- D-592: ActionGate.evaluate_ta_decision added as a new method on Plan 59's ActionGate — rather than modifying GolemRiskEngine (which doesn't know about TaDecision), ActionGate gains a standalone entry point for TA-gated evaluation; the method deserializes TaDecision from DecisionCycleRecord.extensions — Plan 78c
- D-593: Somatic alarm at strength > 0.75 always overrides TA signal direction disagreement — SOMATIC_ALARM_THRESHOLD = 0.75 from Plan 59 (D-318); TA is not trusted over somatic markers at high alarm strength; this is the safety property the integration test verifies — Plan 78c
- D-594: mock_*() constructors added to prior TA types under #[cfg(test)] — test isolation requires controlled subsystem states; production constructors require live chain data; cfg(test) mocks avoid shipping test infrastructure to production builds — Plan 78c
- D-595: LifetimeAggregator confidence = min(count/1000, 1.0) — reaches full confidence at 1000 ticks (~11 hours at theta=40s); a Golem with fewer than 100 ticks has confidence < 0.1 on the lifetime scale — Plan 78c

## Plan 01: Workspace Scaffold & mdbook — Completed 2026-03-19

### Types Defined
- None. Plan 01 creates workspace, crate, app, docs, and sidecar shells only.

### Public Traits
- None.

### Deviations
- Workspace member count is 26, not 25; the scaffold includes all 21 `crates/` members plus 5 `apps/` members, including `apps/bardo-compute/`.
- The root manifest kept the existing extra workspace dependencies used by the real `mirage-rs` code (`jsonrpsee`, `async-trait`, `futures-util`, `lru`, `sysinfo`, `k256`, and `reqwest`'s `blocking` feature) instead of pruning to the narrower plan catalog, because removing them would break the already-implemented app crate.
- `rodio` remains non-optional in `[workspace.dependencies]` because Cargo rejects optional workspace dependencies there; later crates can still opt in locally with `optional = true` if needed.

- `apps/mirage-rs` stayed a real implementation and received a compatibility cleanup for current Axum/jsonrpsee APIs: `{stream_id}` route syntax, a Tower fallback shim, websocket text conversions, public follower visibility, and doctest disablement for the library target.
- This reconciliation pass did not replace any implemented crate or app with shell-only content; the existing scaffold already matched Plan 01 structurally, so the work was verification plus context-file repair.
- `just` is not installed in the current environment, so the `justfile` was validated by inspection and the equivalent `cargo build --workspace` command was used for the build gate.

## Plan 02: Core Types & Event Fabric — Completed 2026-03-19

### Types Defined
- `GolemId` — `golem_core::id` — UUID-backed ephemeral runtime identity
- `GolemConfig` — `golem_core::config` — full `golem.toml` runtime config schema with env overrides
- `GolemError` — `golem_core::error` — crate-wide typed error surface
- `CognitiveTier` — `golem_core::cognitive` — `T0`/`T1`/`T2` inference gating enum
- `CorticalState` — `golem_core::cortical` — cache-aligned lock-free shared perception surface
- `CorticalSnapshot` — `golem_core::cortical` — point-in-time readout of the cortical surface
- `PadVector` — `golem_core::cortical` — `f64` pleasure/arousal/dominance vector
- `BehavioralPhase` — `golem_core::cortical` — vitality phase enum
- `PlutchikEmotion` — `golem_core::cortical` — PAD-octant emotion label enum
- `EventFabric` — `golem_core::event` — broadcast bus plus replay ring buffer
- `GolemEvent` — `golem_core::event` — sequenced typed runtime event
- `EventPayload` — `golem_core::event` — typed event variants across runtime subsystems
- `Subsystem` — `golem_core::event` — event source subsystem enum
- `Extension` — `golem_core::extension` — async hook trait with default no-op implementations
- `ExtensionRegistry` — `golem_core::extension` — validates extension dependencies and dispatch order
- `HookId` — `golem_core::extension` — extension hook identifier enum
- `SessionReason` — `golem_core::extension` — session lifecycle reason
- `InputMessage` / `InputAction` — `golem_core::extension` — input hook request and action types
- `ToolCall` / `ToolAction` / `ToolResult` — `golem_core::extension` — tool hook request, control, and result types
- `AgentMessage` / `SteerMessage` / `OutboundMessage` — `golem_core::extension` — message types for context, steering, and outbound surfaces
- `TaintLabel` — `golem_core::taint` — information-flow label enum
- `TaintedString` — `golem_core::taint` — string paired with a taint label
- `HdcVector` — `golem_core::hdc` — 10,240-bit HDC stub with bind/bundle/permute/similarity
- `TickArena` — `golem_core::alloc` — per-tick `bumpalo` arena wrapper

### Public Traits
- `Extension` — `golem_core::extension` — `fn name(&self) -> &str`, `fn layer(&self) -> u8`, `fn depends_on(&self) -> &[&str]`, plus 21 async lifecycle hooks with default no-op implementations

### Deviations
- `GolemEvent` uses `ts_millis: u64` instead of the export sketch’s `Instant`; this matches the plan’s implementation note because `Instant` is not serializable.
- `GolemConfig` now matches the canonical Plan 02 export set and no longer carries the old `mirage` compatibility field that the scaffold had inherited.
- `ExtensionRegistry::build` performs a dependency-respecting topological sort while preserving layer order and registration order when dependencies do not constrain execution; this is stricter than a pure stable layer sort and still satisfies the plan intent.
- `EventFabric::replay_from` replays inclusively from `after_seq` and keeps the replay ring fixed at 10,000 events.
- `PlutchikEmotion::from_pad(&PadVector::ZERO)` now resolves to `Anticipation`, matching the plan's neutral PAD requirement and fixing the reviewer-reported regression.

### Unresolved Issues
- None.

## Plan 03: mirage-rs — Completed 2026-03-19

### Types Defined
- `MirageSection` — `golem_core::config` — `golem.toml` section for mirage-rs endpoint and retry settings
- `MirageFork` — `mirage_rs::fork` — shared in-process fork handle with resource and event state
- `ForkState` — `mirage_rs::fork` — mutable fork snapshot: HybridDB, block context, receipts, tx store, and execution controls
- `HybridDB` — `mirage_rs::fork` — three-tier state reader: DirtyStore, ReadCache, and upstream RPC
- `DirtyStore` — `mirage_rs::fork` — local mutation layer with snapshots, watch list metadata, and revert support
- `CowState` — `mirage_rs::cow` — copy-on-write storage overlay for speculative and scenario execution
- `BytecodeCache` — `mirage_rs::cow` — LRU bytecode cache keyed by code hash
- `MultiVersionStore` — `mirage_rs::cow` — per-slot multiversion store for optimistic replay tests
- `VersionEntry` — `mirage_rs::cow` — one storage version produced by a transaction incarnation
- `TargetedFollower` — `mirage_rs::replay` — upstream head follower that replays transactions touching watched contracts
- `TxReplay` — `mirage_rs::replay` — fetch-and-replay handle for one upstream transaction hash
- `StateDiff` — `mirage_rs::replay` — canonical transaction diff: accounts, logs, gas, success, output
- `SpeculativeExecutor` — `mirage_rs::replay` — executes requests on cloned fork state without committing writes
- `MirageConfig` — `mirage_rs::integration` — sidecar connection settings derived from defaults or `GolemConfig`
- `MirageClient` — `mirage_rs::integration` — async JSON-RPC client for mirage-rs operations
- `MirageTestInstance` — `mirage_rs::integration` — spawned process wrapper used by integration tests
- `ResourceModel` — `mirage_rs::resources` — profile-driven memory, cache, and watch-list limits
- `ResourceUsage` — `mirage_rs::resources` — runtime usage snapshot with pressure classification inputs
- `ScenarioSet` — `mirage_rs::scenario` — baseline snapshot plus a set of named scenario branches
- `ScenarioRunner` — `mirage_rs::scenario` — sequential and parallel scenario executor over fork state
- `ScenarioResult` — `mirage_rs::scenario` — scenario execution output: status, gas, balances, logs, and comparison metrics
- `ScenarioJob` — `mirage_rs::scenario` — async scenario execution tracker for the RPC API

### Public Traits
- None.

### Deviations
- The existing `mirage-rs` crate already implemented the planned fork, replay, scenario, RPC, and integration surfaces; this plan completed it by validating the full behavior, exposing `TxReplay` from the library root, wiring `MirageConfig::from_golem_config`, and updating documentation/context artifacts rather than replacing the engine wholesale.
- The plan’s specified direct `alloy 1.7.x` and `revm 36` dependency activation was not applied in `mirage-rs` because the workspace is pinned to Rust 1.85.1 while those crates resolve to dependencies requiring Rust 1.88-1.91. The crate therefore retains its current compatible implementation and manifest surface until the workspace toolchain is raised.

### Unresolved Issues
- A full migration from the current compatible engine/provider stack to direct `revm`/Alloy execution should be revisited after the workspace toolchain is upgraded beyond Rust 1.85.
- The plan brief referenced a missing `plans/context/last-completed.md`; this run recreated that handoff file from the verified implementation state.

- cargo test: 94 pass, 0 fail, 0 ignored

## Plan 07: Terminal Protocol Views — Completed 2026-03-21

### Types Defined
- `MockTickRange` — `bardo_terminal::mock::protocol_data` — tick bounds + `position_fraction` / `is_in_range`
- `MockPoolState` — `bardo_terminal::mock::protocol_data` — mock Uniswap-style pool snapshot
- `MockLendingMarket` — `bardo_terminal::mock::protocol_data` — mock lending market snapshot
- `MockVaultState` — `bardo_terminal::mock::protocol_data` — mock ERC-4626 vault snapshot
- `MockBridgeStatus` — `bardo_terminal::mock::protocol_data` — bridge status enum
- `MockBridgeRoute` — `bardo_terminal::mock::protocol_data` — mock bridge route + optional progress fields
- `UniswapPoolWidget` — `bardo_terminal::widgets::protocol_panels` — pool panel (`Widget` impl)
- `LendingMarketWidget` — `bardo_terminal::widgets::protocol_panels` — lending panel (`Widget` impl)
- `BridgeStatusWidget` — `bardo_terminal::widgets::protocol_panels` — bridge panel (`Widget` impl)
- `VaultWidget` — `bardo_terminal::widgets::vault` — vault panel (`Widget` impl)
- `ProtocolViewsScreen` — `bardo_terminal::screens::protocol_views` — `Screen` for protocol grid/stack

### Public Traits
- None added at crate root (binary crate; items are `pub(crate)`).

### Deviations
- **Module layout:** Plan specified `widgets/protocol/{uniswap_pool,lending_market,vault,bridge_status}.rs`; implementation consolidates three panels in `widgets/protocol_panels.rs` and keeps `VaultWidget` in `widgets/vault.rs`.
- **Mock shapes:** Field names differ slightly from the plan Quick Reference (e.g. `base_symbol`/`quote_symbol` vs `token0_symbol`/`token1_symbol`; bridge uses `source_chain`/`dest_chain`, `eta_seconds`, and optional `elapsed_seconds`/`estimated_seconds` for progress instead of separate `now_secs`/`submitted_at_secs` on the widget).
- **Configs:** Plan’s `UniswapPoolWidgetConfig`, `BridgeStatusWidgetConfig`, and `BridgeStatusWidget::with_timing` are not separate types; behavior is folded into struct fields / mock data.
- **Screen title:** `ScreenId::ProtocolViews` maps to short title `"PROTOCOL"` (and tab `"Views"`) in `screen.rs`, not the plan’s literal `"PROTOCOLS"`.
- **Navigation:** Compact layout uses sequential `j`/`k` movement across four cells in addition to arrow keys; `AtomicBool` tracks last render’s compact vs grid mode so key handling matches layout.
- **Serde:** Mock types derive `Serialize`/`Deserialize` for future snapshot export (plan called this optional).

### Unresolved Issues
- `cargo test --workspace` currently fails on `mirage-rs` integration test `integration_snapshot_revert` (balance assertion); unrelated to `bardo-terminal`. Plan 07 verification: `cargo check --workspace` and `cargo test -p bardo-terminal` pass.
- Downstream Plans 70a–70c should replace mocks per `TODO` comments and reconcile field names with real `golem-chain-intelligence` types when those exist.
